source = "arXiv"
summary = "对 Jev 等三种决策模型的评测表明，多正确选项场景中归一化概率存在显著分配不对称，因而固定概率阈值无法可靠识别全部正确选项。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29217"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文评测了 Jev、Liquid d1 和 GPT-6 Luna Decisions 在 MetaTool、HotpotQA 与 ECtHR 三个数据集上的概率分配和阈值决策表现。即使模型将所有正确选项都排在干扰项之前，仍有 28%～50% 的列表包含概率低于均匀基线 1/n 的正确选项。面对同一正确选项的五个完全相同副本，模型给出的概率平均差距达到 17～79 个百分点，暴露出明显的分配不对称。实验进一步表明，这种差异会损害概率阈值对多个正确选项的召回，因此排序正确并不意味着概率可直接解释为正确集合的可靠判据。"
purpose = "- 提醒决策系统不能用单正确答案任务上的校准结果推断多正确选项场景中的概率可靠性。\n- 多标签候选选择应分别评估排序质量、概率分配公平性和阈值召回能力。\n- 下游应用宜采用集合预测、动态阈值或独立置信度估计，避免直接对归一化概率设置固定阈值。\n- 相同选项副本测试可作为诊断概率分配稳定性与对称性的压力测试。"
notes = "标题：When Multiple Choices Are Correct: Asymmetric Probability Allocation in Jev and Other Decision Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29217\n摘要：Decision models return normalized probability distributions over candidate choices, which downstream applications can use to select among choices. Although their providers emphasize the reliability of these probabilities, calibration on tasks with a single correct choice does not establish their reliability when multiple choices are correct. We evaluate three decision models (Jev, Liquid d1, and GPT-6 Luna Decisions) on three diverse datasets (MetaTool, HotpotQA, and ECtHR) to study probability allocation and thresholding. Even when models rank every correct choice above every distractor, in 28% to 50% of those lists, at least one correct choice receives a probability below the uniform probability of 1/n, where n is the number of choices. We also present each model with five identical copi"

content = "TeachBack面向在策略自蒸馏中“教师轨迹准确但未必适合学生学习”的问题，指出完整参考答案可能导致教师省略探索、验证和自我纠错过程。该框架通过控制注入提示的参考推理后缀长度并改变提示风格，引导教师重新探索和求解问题，降低其对特权信息的依赖。每轮轨迹刷新时，共享策略分别生成具有特权信息的教师轨迹和仅基于题目的学生轨迹，再利用正向与反向的成对偏好目标双向优化。数学与逻辑推理实验表明，该方法持续优于现有OPSD方法及相关基线。"
purpose = "- 改善自蒸馏监督轨迹的可学习性，而非仅追求教师答案准确率。\n- 利用学生行为反向塑造教师，使教师轨迹包含更充分的探索、验证与纠错过程。\n- 为特权信息学习、推理轨迹蒸馏和教师监督设计提供双向闭环优化思路。"
notes = "标题：TeachBack: Learning to Teach via Bidirectional Preference Optimization for On-Policy Self-Distillation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29167\n摘要：On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) enables a language model to learn from a Teacher instantiated from the same model but given access to privileged information, such as a reference solution. We find that access to sufficient privileged information allows the Teacher to generate highly accurate trajectories; however, accuracy alone does not guarantee that these trajectories are suitable for Student learning. Direct access to a complete solution may encourage shortened, reference-conditioned derivations that omit the exploration, verification, and self-correction required to solve problems without privileged information. We introduce TeachBack, a bidirectional preference-learning framework for on-policy self-distillation that learns not only from the Teacher but also how the Teacher should t"

summary = "该研究利用父智能体表现与代码差异特征，在实际评测前预测脚手架编辑价值，从而以有限预算搜索并选出更优的自改进编程智能体。"

code = ""

content = "研究针对编程智能体重写自身脚手架时的候选选择问题，要求在编辑尚未完成评测前决定优先扩展哪项修改。方法使用96个脚手架编辑拟合线性读出器，特征包括父节点在引导划分上的解题率及候选代码差异的8维表示，拟合后冻结参数。搜索过程维护包含4个节点的档案，并将执行前编辑评分与基于 Thompson sampling 的 Huxley-Gödel Machine 选择策略进行比较。12次 GPT-4o 实验中，该方法在 HumanEval+ 留出集达到87.0%，高于父节点解题率消融的84.2%、根脚手架的81.2%和校准集最佳编辑的82.8%；Claude Sonnet 4.5 实验也取得86.6%。"

notes = "标题：Recursive Self-Improvement on a Budget: Scoring Code Edits Before They Run\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29121\n摘要：A coding agent that rewrites its own scaffold has to choose which proposed edit to expand before that edit has been evaluated, and the search then has to return one agent. Each run fits one linear readout on the parent's steering-split solve rate and eight coordinates of the proposed diff, from 96 scaffold edits labeled by four evaluations of a 16-instance fit split, and then freezes the coefficients. HumanEval+ reserves 48 instances as the steering split and Polyglot reserves 64. That split scores parents and keeps an archive of four nodes. Huxley-Gödel Machine selection draws a parent by Thompson sampling on the steering counts of nodes that have already been evaluated, and it expands the child that parent proposes. Every method then returns the earliest evaluated node whose steering-spl"

source = "arXiv"
summary = "该研究以单次前向传播读取 logits 或隐藏状态实现类型化决策，用低成本自托管 LoRA 评判模型筛选智能体编程数据，并在 JevBench 上取得可比商业系统的表现。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29052"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- 类型化决策直接将证据、问题和固定选项映射为各选项概率，无需生成并解析文本，可在一次前向传播中完成判断。\n- 内部 System 1 评判器采用冻结骨干、rank-16 LoRA 与 pointer head，对约 14.8 万条智能体编程会话进行训练语料筛选；处理 1,000 条会话约需单卡 3 分钟、成本约 0.06 美元。\n- 相比每轮约 1,000 美元的前沿 LLM 评判器，该方案成本约为其 1%，并可确保会话数据留在组织内部；原有评判标签与新双评判器共识的匹配率仅为 26.9%。\n- 基于约 2.5 万条决策数据训练的开放 LoRA 模型中，35B 版本在 JevBench 的 111 个困难样本上达到 79.3%，与商业参考系统的 73.0% 无显著差异。\n- 消融实验未发现训练输出层 26 个字母行带来明确收益，而语料版本变化使 2B 模型准确率波动 11.7 个百分点，表明小规模评测的不确定性较高。"
purpose = "- 为大规模智能体轨迹筛选提供低延迟、低成本且保护数据隐私的评判方案。\n- 说明固定选项任务可用 logits 或轻量读出头替代文本生成式评判，显著提升吞吐量。\n- 为自托管评判模型、参数高效微调及训练数据策展提供可复用的工程路径。\n- 提醒研究者谨慎解读小样本基准结果，并通过多随机种子、更大评测集和显著性检验验证结论。"

summary = "论文提出基于继承性与置信下界的提示合并算子准入测试，用群体相对评估判断算子是否真正将子代推向供体，并发现追加类算子优于自由重写。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29023"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文以当前种群作为相对评分参照，并用冻结评估面板记录能力提升，以避免固定验证器饱和及种子淘汰造成的虚假进步。只有最终平均表现超过初代最佳成员，实验运行才被视为产生了有效训练。准入测试要求合并后的子代相对零剂量基线向供体方向移动，且该移动的置信下界至少达到请求剂量的一半；可检测的不足会导致拒绝，否则结果为未确定。在 Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite 的谈判任务上，策略追加与约束重写通过测试，自由重写因仅实现约三分之一的目标移动而被拒绝，模板追加则因复测不稳定而保持未确定。"
purpose = "- 为提示合并和进化搜索提供比最终任务得分更严格的算子准入标准，验证子代是否真正继承了供体特征。\n- 通过零剂量对照、置信下界和留出复测，区分真实继承、随机波动与种群淘汰造成的表面提升。\n- 实验表明，追加短规则等局部编辑可能比自由重写更稳定，适合用于智能体提示的迭代演化。\n- 相对种群评分可缓解固定验证器饱和问题，但仍需在更多任务、模型和固定预言机对照下验证泛化性。"
notes = "标题：Inheritance as an Admission Test for LLM Prompt Merge Operators\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29023\n摘要：Large language model (LLM) agents are often improved by iterating until a fixed verifier is done. Once that verifier saturates, the loop has nothing left to climb. We instead score agents against the current population, and a frozen panel records whether anyone got better. The panel mean can rise inside the seeds by elimination alone. We therefore count a run as training only when its final mean beats the best generation-zero member. A merge is admitted only when the child moves toward the donor, past the child the same operator produces at zero dose, and the lower confidence bound on that movement reaches half the requested dose. That is the admission test. A detectable shortfall rejects the operator, and the test is otherwise undetermined. We keep realised fitness φas the selector and re"


[[items]]

summary = "VeriEvolve先合成可执行评估器与挑战状态，再以冻结评估器作为适应度信号驱动工具智能体工作流演化，并通过反事实测试持续校准评估层。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29005"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "VeriEvolve针对有状态真实任务缺少可靠适应度信号的问题，提出“评估器优先”的智能体演化框架。系统根据任务指令合成挑战状态及可执行评估器，在每轮工作流演化期间冻结评估器，并将其结果作为搜索的适应度信号；轮次之间则利用强、弱执行轨迹的终态构造反事实测试以修订评估器。在30项MCPMark文件系统任务上，框架生成了30个评估器和141个挑战状态，对生成测试套件实现141/141一致，对执行智能体产生的留出状态实现113/122一致。评估器引导的演化将GLM-5.2的外部验证成功数从6/30提升至18/30，将Claude从27/30提升至30/30，并在跨环境实验中揭示了可观测性、状态表征及搜索与执行能力的限制。"
purpose = "- 为状态可观测且具备外部验证器的工具调用任务提供可执行、可校准的适应度信号。\n- 将评估器构建置于智能体优化之前，可降低不可靠自动评判导致的奖励作弊或错误演化风险。\n- 利用强弱执行终态生成反事实测试，为评估器与智能体交替演化提供闭环机制。\n- 跨文件系统、PostgreSQL、Playwright、Notion和GitHub环境的结果表明，落地时需优先检查状态可观测性与表征完备性。"
notes = "标题：VeriEvolve: Evaluator-First Evolution for Tool-Using Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29005\n摘要：A central obstacle to self-improving agents is the absence of a reliable fitness signal for realistic stateful tasks. We introduce \\textsc{VeriEvolve}, an evaluator-first evolution framework that constructs this missing evaluation layer before optimizing the agent. From task instruction, VeriEvolve synthesizes instruction-grounded challenge states and an executable evaluator that checks them. During each round of workflow evolution, the evaluator is frozen and serves as the fitness signal. Between rounds, VeriEvolve revises the evaluator by converting the end states of strong and weak runs into a pair of counterfactual test cases. Across all 30 MCPMark Filesystem tasks, VeriEvolve constructs 30 evaluators with 141 challenge states, achieving 141/141 agreement on the generated suites and 11"

summary = "研究对比长文档 RAG 的扫描、稠密检索与混合重排策略，发现全量扫描并不必然保留更多证据或生成更准确的答案，且延迟与复用成本更高。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28957"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究在统一段落、最多五段证据和固定答案生成器的条件下，对比了词法搜索、稠密检索、混合重排及全文扫描等 11 种配置。评测覆盖 396 个 QASPER 问题，以及来自八本完整教材的 80 个问题。教材实验中，Hybrid + Jev 的证据覆盖率最高，达到 94.1%，高于 Jev 全文扫描的 88.7% 和 OpenAI 稠密检索的 81.3%。然而，证据覆盖率与答案准确率并不稳定相关，Hybrid + Decisions 以 0.892 获得最高教材答案准确率。全文扫描还带来显著延迟，而随着查询复用增加，稠密检索的预计 API 成本低于付费决策式方案。"
purpose = "- 为长文档 RAG 的检索策略选择提供证据覆盖、答案质量、延迟和累计成本四个互补维度。\n- 说明全文扫描仍可能在后续压缩或筛选阶段丢失相关证据，不能将“看过全部段落”等同于“保留全部证据”。\n- 提示实际系统不应只优化召回率，还需直接评估最终答案准确率及多次查询下的索引复用收益。\n- 对能够被混合候选池覆盖的中等长度文档，全文扫描的边际收益可能十分有限。"
notes = "标题：Scan or Index? large Evidence Coverage, Answer Quality, and Cost in Long-Document RAG\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28957\n摘要：Scanning every passage in a long document seems thorough, but does it preserve more useful evidence or produce better answers? We compare 11 retrieval configurations spanning lexical and dense search, hybrid reranking, and full-document scanning with Jev, OpenAI Decisions, and Cloudflare Clef. Our evaluation uses 396 QASPER questions and 80 questions from eight complete textbooks, with common passages, a five-passage limit, and a fixed answer generator. On books, Hybrid + Jev achieves the highest observed evidence coverage (94.1%), compared with 88.7% for Jev full scan and 81.3\\% for OpenAI dense retrieval. Full scans examine every passage initially, yet later reduction steps can discard relevant evidence. Higher evidence coverage also does not consistently produce better answers: Hybrid +"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28945"

content = "该框架在每个决策步骤生成多个候选动作，并使用世界模型想象后续结果，从而在不实际执行的情况下比较不同方案。针对语言环境缺少显式动力学与成本函数的问题，系统依据任务进展和风险评估预测结果，再以 MPPI 风格的成本权重指导最终决策。双分支经验记忆分别服务于规划器和世界模型：前者优化候选生成与动作选择，后者改善下一观测预测。该方法在 ALFWorld 和 ScienceWorld 上使用三种骨干模型进行实验，展现出较强的整体表现及经验引导预测控制的有效性。"
purpose = "- 将模型预测控制思想引入语言智能体，为测试时规划提供多分支、低环境交互成本的行动筛选机制。\n- 通过积累部署期间的交互经验，同时改进规划器与世界模型，实现无需更新环境动力学定义的持续自我改进。\n- 适用于需要控制执行风险和试错成本的长程任务，可为智能体的安全决策与经验复用提供参考。"
notes = "标题：Self-Improving Agents with World Model Predictive Paths\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28945\n摘要：Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly expected to interact with environments and improve their decisions during deployment. However, how anticipating future outcomes and drawing on accumulated interaction experience can jointly support decision-making remains underexplored. We propose a test-time framework, inspired by Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control, that combines multi-branch predictive analysis with experience-based adaptation. At each step, the agent proposes multiple candidate actions and imagines their outcomes with a world model, so that alternatives can be compared without being executed in the environment. Since language environments provide neither explicit dynamics nor costs, the predicted outcomes are evaluated for task progress and risk, and MPPI-sty"

summary = "DDF结合信息冗余、成本归一化边际效用、统计显著性与治理因素，在分阶段智能体管线中评估企业候选数据资产的增量价值。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28933"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "DDF将mRMR的“相关性减冗余”原则从特征层扩展至数据资产层，以互信息衡量候选资产相对于既有数据组合的冗余度。框架通过消融实验计算经成本归一化且受统计显著性门控的边际效用，并纳入信任度、身份解析质量和战略权重等业务治理输入。SDSAE分阶段执行评估，对未通过治理或冗余门控的数据资产提前终止昂贵的模型重训练。在信用卡欺诈基准上的实验中，仅真正具有差异化信息的资产获得采购建议，且结果在多数战略权重配置和给定决策阈值范围内保持稳定。"
purpose = "- 为企业采购或引入外部数据提供可量化的增量价值评估依据。\n- 通过前置治理与冗余门控减少无效模型重训练，降低数据评估成本。\n- 将信息论指标、统计检验和业务治理要求统一到可审计的决策流程中。\n- 可用于构建候选数据资产排序、采购推荐和数据组合优化系统。"
notes = "标题：The Data Differentiator Framework (DDF): Quantifying Marginal Data Value with Information-Theoretic and Game-Theoretic Measures in an Agentic Evaluation Pipeline (SDSAE)\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28933\n摘要：In data-rich enterprises the hard question is not whether a candidate dataset is good in isolation, but whether it adds information beyond what the organization already holds, and whether that increment is worth its price. We present the Data Differentiator Framework (DDF), a composite scoring method for candidate data assets that combines (i) a mutual-information-based redundancy penalty against the existing portfolio, (ii) a cost-normalized, significance-gated marginal utility score derived from an ablation study, and (iii) explicit governance inputs (trust, identity resolution quality, strategic weights) set by the business. The framework is executed by a staged pipeline, the Strategic Data Sourcing Agent Ecosystem (SDSAE), that short-circuits expensive model-retraining steps for assets"

content = "D-Skill将教师模型视为黑盒，通过比较教师与学生的配对执行轨迹，识别行为分歧与共识并提炼经验。系统使用兼容的技能更新后端，在离线阶段把经验整合为自然语言技能文档，供学生在推理时读取。该过程基于固定轨迹完成，不需要标签、额外环境交互或模型权重更新。在SpreadsheetBench-Verified、ALFWorld和国际象棋谜题上，D-Skill弥合了75%至95%的师生能力差距，并优于单轮SFT、接近多轮SFT；同模型家族下，学生的单任务推理成本比教师低5.7至8.3倍。"
purpose = "- 为无法访问学生权重或缺少训练算力的场景提供黑盒能力迁移方案。\n- 将隐式参数知识转化为可检查、可编辑和可复用的自然语言技能，提高蒸馏过程的可解释性。\n- 可用于以较低推理成本将前沿智能体能力迁移至小型模型，并作为SFT等权重训练方案的替代或补充。\n- 配对轨迹中的行为分歧可作为技能提炼信号，减少对人工标签和新增环境交互的依赖。"
notes = "标题：D-Skill: Distilling Teacher Knowledge through Agent Skills\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28914\n摘要：Frontier language-model agents excel at complex tasks, but high inference costs make transferring their capabilities to smaller models essential. Traditional knowledge distillation achieves this through parameter updates, requiring access to the student's weights and additional training compute while encoding the transferred knowledge implicitly in the parameters. We introduce D-Skill, a framework that treats the teacher as a black box and distills its behavioral advantage into an interpretable natural-language skill by analyzing paired rollouts and identifying behavioral divergences and agreements. The resulting lessons are consolidated offline, with any compatible skill-update backend, into a skill document that is provided to the student at inference time. D-Skill constructs the skill e"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Skill Schema Transport（SST）将新领域知识重构为模型已有技能空间中的可复用程序，在持续领域适应中同时降低灾难性遗忘并改善相关任务泛化。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28892"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出 Skill Schema Transport（SST），不再通过限制参数漂移或回放旧数据来维持原有能力，而是将新领域知识映射至模型已有的技能空间。SST 将不同样例类型抽象为技能图式，保留关键操作、依赖关系和验证步骤，同时去除领域特定的表面形式。该框架由此把知识更新从训练轨迹记忆转化为已有程序的复用。在两个 3B 指令微调模型上，SST 将流式领域的平均最终性能提高 2.53～5.75 分，并在 Qwen-3B MATH 数据流上将最大性能下降从 42.41 分降至 7.10 分。"
purpose = "- 为语言模型的连续后训练提供一种区别于数据回放、输出保持和参数正则化的抗遗忘路径。\n- 可通过技能图式抽象，将跨领域样例转换为模型熟悉的操作程序，提升知识更新效率与迁移能力。\n- 对需要持续接入新领域数据的系统，可将最大性能下降和分布外能力恢复纳入核心评估指标。"
notes = "标题：Combating Catastrophic Forgetting in Continual Domain Adaptation via Knowledge Recasting\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28892\n摘要：Adapting language models to new domains during post-training risks degrading previously acquired capabilities, a phenomenon commonly known as catastrophic forgetting. Existing mitigation methods largely treat forgetting as a consequence of excessive model drift. They therefore constrain updates by preserving old outputs, replaying old data, or regularizing changes to parameters and representations so that the updated model remains close to its previous state. We take a different view: Rather than pulling the updated model back toward its old state, we translate the new domain into the model's pre-existing knowledge space, making unfamiliar knowledge learnable through familiar procedures. Therefore, we propose Skill Schema Transport (SST), a post-training framework that transports new knowl"

link = ""
content = "研究构建了一支由不同学科背景智能体组成的持久化 AI 科研团队，以正交探索降低架构搜索陷入局部最优的风险。每个智能体保留私有研究历史，同时与团队共享实验结果，并通过自适应机制获得提出和执行实验的机会。该团队被用于搜索线性注意力架构，所得架构在 1.3B 参数规模下的语言建模、常识推理以及合成和真实检索任务上优于 GDN2。消融实验表明，学科多样性、私有历史和自适应资源分配均是持续取得发现的关键组织组件。"
purpose = "- 为有限实验预算下的自主科学发现提供兼顾探索与利用的多智能体组织方案。\n- 表明科研智能体的长期私有记忆能够在共享证据相同的情况下促进累积式创新。\n- 可用于神经网络架构搜索、自动实验设计及其他高成本科学探索任务。\n- 提示提升自主发现能力不仅依赖更强的基础智能体，也依赖团队构成、记忆机制和资源分配策略。"
notes = "标题：Building AI Research Teams for Autonomous Discovery of Linear Attention Architectures\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28887\n摘要：Autonomous agents can increasingly propose ideas, modify code, and run experiments, creating an opportunity to accelerate scientific discovery using teams of collaborative AI researchers. To escape local minima in the discovery, we propose to encourage orthogonal exploration by constructing the team using agents with varying expertise. However, realizing this design requires more than diverse backgrounds. When experiments are costly, a team can test only a limited number of ideas, thereby creating the need to balance exploration and exploitation when selecting a subset of researchers to propose and execute new experiments. Furthermore, each result should shape what the team tries next. We therefore ask how AI researchers with different scientific backgrounds can build on one another's find"

content = "SANE 将随机评估器的行为要求编码为可复用的智能体指令，并依据评分器规范、实现及代表性输入识别其特定能力。系统自动推导扰动方案及预期分数关系，同时以独立于评分器的验证框架检查方案是否符合评分意图、测试实例是否正确实现扰动。跨五个领域的实验中，SANE 生成的有效测试族数量达到 Judge Reliability Harness 的 3.7 倍，并在所有领域发现评分器缺陷。在忠实度评测上，其测试套件对 11 个评判模型的排名与 FaithBench 人工标注结果高度一致，Spearman 相关系数为 0.852。"

notes = "标题：SANE: Automated Scorer-Specific Metamorphic Testing for LLM Judges\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28886\n摘要：LLM judges increasingly mediate benchmark evaluation, model selection, data curation, and training. Their decisions are useful only when each judge responds reliably to changes affecting its scoring criterion. Existing methods for reliability testing perturb inputs with known expected effects on judge scores. This limits their use to fixed perturbations and pre-assumed scorer types. We introduce SANE, an agent-based framework that encodes behavioral requirements for stochastic evaluators as reusable agent instructions. Given a scorer specification, implementation, and representative inputs, the pipeline fir"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28834"
code = ""

content = "论文提出 Banzhaf Resource Attribution（BRA），在固定失败状态与修订协议的条件下，以受控因子实验衡量计算、信息和反馈三类推理时资源的边际贡献及交互作用。作者在 MBPP+、HumanEval+ 的 194 个函数级失败案例和 SWE-bench Verified 的 126 个仓库级失败案例上评估了六个模型。结果显示，任务相关规格能稳定提升修复率，公开测试反馈仅带来小幅收益，检索代码几乎没有帮助。随着模型能力增强，额外计算的价值下降，但信息仍然有效；同时，多个前沿模型几乎总是选择反馈资源，其收益甚至低于均匀随机选择。"
purpose = "- 为推理时资源分配提供因果测量框架，避免将 inference-time scaling 简化为单一的词元预算扩展。\n- 编程智能体陷入失败状态时，应优先补充任务相关规格等高价值信息，而非盲目增加计算或测试反馈。\n- 可利用 BRA 审计智能体的资源选择策略，识别主观偏好与实际修复收益之间的偏差。\n- 对更强模型而言，系统优化重点可能需要从增加推理计算转向信息检索、资源路由与选择策略校准。"
notes = "标题：Where Should a Stuck Agent Spend Its Next Tokens? Causal Attribution of Inference-Time Resources\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28834\n摘要：Inference-time scaling is often treated as a token-budget problem, but a failed coding agent can seek qualitatively different resources: computation, information, or feedback. We introduce Banzhaf Resource Attribution (BRA), a controlled factorial framework that measures each resource's marginal contribution to repair and its interactions while holding the failed state and revision protocol fixed. We evaluate six models on 194 function-level failures from MBPP+ and HumanEval+ and 126 repository-level failures from SWE-bench Verified. Task-relevant specifications consistently improve repair, whereas public-test feedback provides small gains and retrieved code little to none. The value of additional computation declines with model capability, while information remains useful; resource channe"

content = "论文比较了可输出结构化判断的决策语言模型 Jev 与前沿及开放权重 LLM 评判器在四类临床评测场景中的表现。在 118 个儿科答案上，Jev 与医生有序质量评分的一致性达到 Gwet's AC2 0.50，匹配或超过 12 个 LLM 评判器中的 7 个。在 HealthBench、MedHELM 和 HealthBench Professional 上，Jev 的判断一致性也接近或达到强基线水平，其中与 GPT-5.4 参考评判器在 91% 的标准上相符。相较 Jev，前沿 LLM 评判器成本高出 73–294 倍，耗时高出 7–57 倍，显示其适合大规模临床 AI 自动评估。"

notes = "标题：Decision language models as affordable and rapid alternatives to LLM judges of clinical generative AI\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28826\n摘要：Automated judges of clinical artificial intelligence (AI) outputs are necessary, but large language model (LLM) judges are costly and slow at scale. We compared Jev, a decision language model that returns structured judgments, with frontier and open-weight LLM judges in four use cases. On 118 pediatric answers, Jev's agreement with physicians' ordinal quality ratings (Gwet's AC2 0.50; expected score 0.53) matched or exceeded that of seven of 12 LLM judges, including frontier models, Claude Opus 5.5 (0.49) and GPT-6 (0.38). On physician-graded binary HealthBench criteria, Jev matched or exceeded four of six LLM judges. On MedHELM's open-ended tasks, Jev's agreement with a three-LLM jury approached or exceeded the jurors' internal agreement. On HealthBench Professional, Jev matched the bench"

summary = "Successive Scale Racing（SSR）通过自适应筛选任务执行证据组合，使智能体脚手架在任务池扩大时持续提升性能，并减少60%的验证执行。"

content = "智能体脚手架演化利用任务执行经验，持续优化智能体的指令、工具与控制流。研究发现，现有方法会因证据选择不佳而在任务池扩大时出现性能下降，不同证据组合可能导致显著不同的演化结果。SSR依据任务质量与覆盖范围构造多样化证据组合，并在有限计算预算下竞赛评估候选脚手架。在三个基准的六种配置中，SSR均取得最佳结果；任务数从40增至200时，通过率由64.3%升至68.3%，同时验证执行量减少60%。"
purpose = "- 揭示任务规模扩大并不必然改善脚手架演化，证据组合质量是影响结果的关键因素。\n- 可将SSR用于智能体指令、工具配置和控制流的自动化联合优化。\n- 通过小预算候选竞赛减少验证成本，适合任务池持续扩张的智能体开发流程。\n- 为脚手架演化系统设计任务质量与覆盖率兼顾的证据选择策略提供参考。"
notes = "标题：Forging More Tasks into Better Harnesses: Agentic Harness Evolution via Successive Scale Racing\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28815\n摘要：Agentic harness evolution optimizes an agent's instructions, tools, and control flow by using its task execution experiences as evolving evidence. While more evolution tasks should intuitively improve performance, our preliminary study reveals a paradoxical failure mode: the performance of existing methods degrades as the task pool expands. Our further analysis demonstrates that this degradation stems from suboptimal evidence selection during optimization, as different evidence combinations lead to substantially different evolution outcomes. To address this issue, we propose Successive Scale Racing (SSR), a framework that adaptively searches for optimal evidence combinations in each evolution round. SSR constructs diverse evidence combinations based on the corresponding task quality and co"

summary = "Discord 与 BRACE 利用奖励分歧、历史任务证据及相关奖励建模，提前预测具有混合奖励的任务组，从而将有限 Rollout 预算分配到更具训练信号的 MCP 智能体交互中。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28785"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文指出，同一 Rollout 组内奖励完全一致时无法提供组内相对学习信号，而工具交互本身成本高昂。Discord 通过严格配对推理和保持决策不变的早停机制评估智能体脚手架修改，仅由结果不一致的样本判定修改优劣。BRACE 综合历史任务库、任务成功率不确定性与相关奖励模型，在生成前预测可能出现混合奖励的任务组并据此分配预算。在三个 Qwen3-VL 规模上的 MCP 实验中，BRACE 使用更少的新训练 Rollout 超越基线峰值，并在未经额外训练的 BFCL 和 NESTFUL 评测上取得领先结果。"
purpose = "- 将 Rollout 优先分配给预计能够产生奖励对比的任务，减少无有效相对信号的交互开销。\n- 可用 Discord 对智能体脚手架或 Harness 修改进行低偏差配对评估，并借助早停降低测试成本。\n- BRACE 展示了历史任务库与不确定性校准在强化学习样本调度中的价值，适合昂贵工具调用环境。\n- 实践中需同时核算历史任务库采集成本，并权衡样本保留收益与额外存储、评估开销。"
notes = "标题：Measure First, Then Allocate: Calibrated Rollout Budgets for MCP Agents in Reinforcement Learning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28785\n摘要：Training tool-using language models requires costly interaction, yet identical rewards within a rollout group provide no group-relative learning signal. We first introduce Discord, which tests harness edits using exact paired inference and decision-preserving early stopping: only disagreeing paired outcomes favor one harness over another. This insight motivates prospective allocation in BRACE (Bank-calibrated Rollout Allocation under Correlated Episodes). BRACE combines historical task evidence, uncertainty in task success rates, and a correlated reward model to forecast mixed-reward groups before allocating generation budget. Controlled ha"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28770"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "ReAPO针对RLVR独立随机采样容易反复生成相似失败策略的问题，将错误解纳入后续尝试的上下文以开展串行探索。该方法从随机最短路径视角建模到达可验证成功响应的累计成本，并据此对GRPO进行简单修改。ReAPO为失败轨迹分配额外生成机会，只强化成功的重试而不奖励此前失败，从而实现更合理的尝试级信用分配。在Qwen3和OLMo-2的0.6B至4B模型及八项数学与科学推理基准上，ReAPO优于GRPO等基线；在Qwen3-0.6B上达到对照峰值准确率时，生成词元分别减少39%和31%。"

notes = "标题：ReAPO: Retry-Aware Policy Optimization for Efficient Exploration in LLM Reasoning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28770\n摘要：RLVR improves language-model reasoning, but commonly relies on independent stochastic rollouts for exploration, which can repeatedly sample similar failed strategies. We show that, under suitable simplifying assumptions, this perspective naturally lea"

content = "Agent4Sci 是面向科学机器学习的长程智能体测试平台，覆盖 11 个领域、20 项基准，要求智能体改进已有最佳公开模型。当前前沿智能体在所有基准上均取得提升，其中 Claude Opus 的平均相对提升为 10.4%，且未发现学术不端行为。轨迹分析表明，这些进展主要来自常规机器学习工程技巧，而非真正的领域研究洞见，并暴露出新颖性校准不足、资源利用不充分及快速陷入局部工程搜索等问题。加入研究构思、实验管理和定时检查清单等轻量插件技能后，Claude Opus 的提升增至 20.1%，Codex 则由 4.4% 增至 8.1%，同时产生了更多样且更贴合领域的干预方案。"
purpose = "- 为评估科研智能体在真实、长程科学机器学习任务中的实际进展提供统一测试平台。\n- 揭示基准分数提升可能主要源于工程调优，不能直接等同于研究级创新或领域洞见。\n- 表明研究构思、实验管理和检查清单等脚手架技能可显著改善智能体的实验效率与方案多样性。\n- 可用于追踪前沿智能体从自动化机器学习工程向自主科学研究能力演进的过程。"
notes = "标题：Automating ML for Science: Can Frontier Agents Climb Scientific Hills in the Wild?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28763\n摘要：Frontier coding agents are increasingly framed as end-to-end automated researchers, and ML for science offers a demanding test of that promise: improving a scientific model often requires domain insight beyond routine ML engineering. We introduce Agent4Sci, a long-horizon testbed for measuring how far today's agents can improve the published best models on scientific ML benchmarks, spanning 20 benchmarks across 11 domains. Rather than ranking agents on a leaderboard, Agent4Sci asks what kinds of progress agents can make on these tasks under in-the-wild conditions, and where they fall short. Empirically, today's frontier agents advance the published best on every benchmark, with Claude Opus reaching 10.4% mean relative improvement and no run flagged for academic misconduct. These gains, how"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28441"

content = "SSA 将高分词元的确定性检索与残余注意力的分数引导采样统一起来，并依据样本入选概率进行校正。该框架涵盖 top-k、top-p 以及 top-k 加采样等既有方案，在相同稀疏度下于 RULER、LongBench、LOFT 和 AIME 上持续优于基线。在 RULER-HARD 的 50 倍注意力稀疏设置中，Ministral-3-8B 在 32K 和 128K 上分别恢复稠密注意力性能的 94% 和 85%。配合优化的 CUDA 内核，SSA 在 100 万词元上下文中相对 FlashInfer 获得最高 2.96 倍解码加速。"
purpose = "- 为超长上下文推理提供兼顾注意力覆盖率、估计方差与计算开销的稀疏化方案。\n- 表明利用代理注意力分数进行分层采样，可显著缩小极端稀疏注意力与稠密注意力之间的精度差距。\n- 可用于降低大型 KV Cache 的访问成本，并为长上下文服务系统的 CUDA 内核优化提供实现参考。"
notes = "标题：Stratified Sparse Attention: Reducing the Sparse-to-Dense Gap at Extreme Sparsity\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28441\n摘要：Large Language Model inference over long contexts is increasingly bottlenecked by the cost of attending to a growing Key-Value (KV) cache. Sparse attention reduces this cost by accessing only a fraction of the cache, but existing methods face a trade-off between deterministic retrieval, which captures high-scoring tokens but discards residual attention mass, and sampling, which improves coverage at the cost of higher variance. Hybrid methods combine top-$k$ retrieval with uniform sampling, yet still exhibit a substantial gap from dense attention at aggressive sparsity. We introduce \\emph{Stratified Sparse Attention (SSA)}, a unified framework combining deterministic retrieval with score-guided sampling. SSA deterministically attends to high-scoring tokens while sampling the residual using "

summary = "SimHack通过少量小规模参考RL实验模拟奖励作弊的训练动态，在正式训练前预测作弊占据主导的时间并筛选低风险训练方案。"

content = "奖励作弊的初始发生率不足以判断其何时在强化学习训练中占据主导，不同训练方案及重复实验之间存在显著差异。SimHack从少量小规模参考RL运行中学习奖励作弊的训练动态，无需重新训练即可为未见过的目标方案生成反事实模拟。该系统预测作弊爆发中位步骤的相对误差为11.3%，跨方案排序相关系数达到0.85，并能泛化到模型规模、课程、采样次数和学习率的变化。用于筛选RL课程时，SimHack发现了在相同场景集合上可将无作弊训练时长延长96.6%的课程方案。"
purpose = "- 在执行高成本RL训练前预估奖励作弊风险，减少无效或危险实验。\n- 支持对候选训练课程和超参数方案进行预筛选，优先采用作弊爆发更晚的配置。\n- 表明奖励作弊治理可从训练期检测与干预，前移至基于反事实模拟的训练方案设计。"
notes = "标题：SimHack: Predicting the onset of detectable reward hacking before RL training\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28707\n摘要：When training LLMs to code with reinforcement learning, reward hacking is often detectable but difficult to prevent, since (a) environment hardening is increasingly difficult as training environments grow more complex; and (b) training-time interventions incentivize models to obfuscate hacking behavior to bypass detection. We instead study whether we can predict when detectable reward hacking becomes dominant before executing an RL run, enabling us to select lower-risk recipes in advance. We find that the initial hacking rate alone does not determine onset, which varies substantially across training recipes and repeated runs. We thus formulate this problem as predicting the distribution of the reward hacking onset step under a target training recipe. We introduce SimHack, a simulator that "

score = 3

summary = "SpecEvo 通过轻量模型持续探索并整合搜索证据、前沿模型稀疏介入关键决策，在保持或提升科学发现效果的同时将 API 成本最多降低 80%。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28706"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "SpecEvo 是一个多 LLM 推测式演化框架，通过按能力和成本分工来提升科学与算法发现效率。轻量模型驱动并行 Speculator 生成和改进候选程序，Advisor 则将成功、失败、重复及趋于饱和的探索方向提炼为持久指导。前沿模型 Navigator 仅在稀疏检查点审视演化种群，并根据搜索停滞情况执行综合、局部精修或问题重构。在覆盖四个科学与算法发现领域的 176 项任务上，该框架达到或超过前沿模型基线，API 成本最多降低 80%，方程发现评估效率最高提升至 1.5 倍，并较 CO-Bench 最强基线提高 3.8%。"
purpose = "- 为自动化科学发现提供“低成本广泛探索、高能力稀疏干预”的多模型协作范式。\n- 表明失败、冗余和饱和试验并非无效开销，可被持续整合为后续搜索的轨迹条件证据。\n- 可用于设计按搜索状态动态路由模型的进化搜索系统，避免对所有候选统一使用昂贵推理。\n- 为方程发现、程序搜索及其他高评估成本任务提供兼顾性能与 API 成本的实现思路。"
notes = "标题：SpecEvo: Speculative Evolution with Large Language Models for Cost-Efficient Scientific Discovery\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28706\n摘要：LLM-driven evolutionary search is a promising paradigm for scientific discovery, but existing methods remain costly and slow, often applying expensive reasoning uniformly while underusing evidence from failed, redundant, and saturated trials. We introduce Speculative Evolution (SpecEvo), a multi-LLM framework that coordinates lightweight and frontier models through continual consolidation of search evidence. Lightweight models power both parallel Speculators, which generate and refine candidate programs, and an Advisor, which distills accumulated successes, failures, and saturated directions into persistent guidance for subsequent exploration. At sparse checkpoints, a frontier-model Navigator inspects the evolving population and intervenes through synthesis, surgical refinement, or reframi"

summary = "PhysiCalWorld通过将测试场景中的探索性交互片段编码为物理描述符，在无微调和无物理标签条件下校准世界模型的实例级动力学。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30125"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "PhysiCalWorld针对质量分布、关节阻尼和表面摩擦等隐含物理属性难以从单帧或短历史中识别的问题，为动作条件世界模型引入上下文物理校准。该方法将测试场景中的探索性交互片段编码为紧凑的物理描述符 token，并以此约束后续预测，使其贴合当前实例的真实动力学。系统仅使用潜变量预测损失进行端到端训练，不依赖物理属性标签、解析物理建模或测试时微调。实验覆盖四类具有不同物理变化的环境，在滚动预测精度和视觉规划成功率上均取得显著提升，且物理描述符能够泛化至训练范围之外。"
purpose = "- 为世界模型适应未知质量、阻尼和摩擦等场景特定动力学提供低成本方案。\n- 探索性交互只需编码一次，所得描述符可跨多次 rollout 复用，适合长程规划与在线控制。\n- 表明无需显式物理监督，也能通过预测目标学习具有物理结构且支持外推的潜在表征。\n- 可用于降低世界模型在新场景中向训练先验回归所导致的累计预测漂移。"
notes = "标题：PhysiCalWorld: In-Context Physics Calibration for World Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30125\n摘要：Existing world models typically perceive a scene through current visual frame or a narrow history window. However, these observations may not reveal the physics properties that determine transition dynamics, such as an object's mass distribution, joint damping, or surface friction. Without calibrating these physics factors for world models, predictions tend to regress toward an empirical prior and induce severe drift in novel test scenes. To this end, we propose PhysiCalWorld, which calibrates action-conditioned world models to the instance-specific dynamics via in-context prompting, without fine-tuning or analytical physics modeling. Our method encodes an exploratory interaction clip collected within the test scene into a compact physics descriptor token, which grounds subsequent predicti"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究者构建 AI-greement 领域专用智能体框架，自动完成共识协议的发现、实现、实验与形式化验证，并由此得到显著降低地理复制状态机延迟的 A-greement 协议。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30114"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究提出面向共识协议研究的智能体框架 AI-greement，整合概念导向的协议知识库、模块化实现骨架以及基于 Lean 的验证框架。该框架发现了新协议 A-greement，通过租约授权的本地读取、客户端直接学习领导者排序写入，以及依据网络拓扑和负载选择的固定法定人数逐键快速写入路径来降低延迟。在六种、包含 5 或 9 个副本的部署中，该协议相较最快基线将写密集和读密集负载的平均延迟分别降低 12.2% 和 33.3%，并在只读负载上达到最快基线水平。Lean 形式化还揭示了既有共识协议中若干此前未报告的漏洞，但作者强调人类专家在搜索前、中、后仍不可替代。"
purpose = "- 展示领域知识库、实现脚手架、实验系统与形式化验证器组合后，可将通用编程智能体转化为自动化系统研究工具。\n- 为共识协议及其他分布式系统机制的候选生成、性能筛选和安全性证明提供闭环工作流参考。\n- 表明形式化验证不仅能约束智能体生成方案，还可用于审计既有协议并发现潜在安全漏洞。\n- 提醒自动化科研更适合重塑专家角色，而非在缺少人工判断的情况下独立完成协议研究。"
notes = "标题：The End of Consensus Research?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30114\n摘要：Consensus is a fundamental problem in distributed computing and at the core of state machine replication (SMR). Despite decades of active human research, discovering practical low-latency solutions to the problem is still open, as it requires combining protocol knowledge, correctness reasoning, implementation, and experimental evaluation. We report on how AI helped us discover A-greement, a new consensus protocol for geo-replicated state machines that significantly outperforms state-of-the-art solutions. Averaged across six deployments with 5 or 9 replicas, A-greement reduces the mean latency of a geo-distributed key-value store over the fastest baseline by 12.2% and 33.3% on write-heavy and read-heavy workloads, respectively, and matches the fastest baseline on read-only workloads. A-gree"

content = "论文将多模态具身智能体定义为结合多模态任务推理、物理行动和反馈修正的目标导向系统。作者提出 PAPAV 框架，将其能力拆分为感知当前状态、预判行动影响、规划可行路径、通过接口或身体行动，以及验证执行结果。该框架按功能而非具体架构组织，可用于比较多模态智能体、机器人系统及二者融合后的具身智能体。对 64 个基准的分析显示，现有评测主要关注行动能力，仅 5 个基准评估预判、3 个评估验证，难以完整反映物理环境中的智能体可靠性。"

notes = "标题：Multimodal Embodied Agents: A Unified Capability-centric Perspective from Computer-Use to Robot-Use\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30108\n摘要：Multimodal agents (MMAs) sustain interaction through reasoning, memory, tools, and feedback, most visibly as computer-use agents, while robotic systems (RSs) couple sensing and actuation under physical dynamics. We define multimodal embodied agents (MMEAs) as goal-directed systems that couple multimodal task reasoning with physical action and revise decisions from the resulting feedback. This raises a central question: what changes when a multimodal agent moves from computer-use to robot-use? We introduce PAPAV, a unified capability-centric framework of five recurring functions: Perceive the current state, Anticipate action effects, Plan a feasible course, Act through an interface or body, and Verify the outcome. Defined by function rather than architecture, these capabilities let PAPAV id"

link = ""
content = "ASTEROID是一种非对称学生—教师监督学习框架，面向部分可观测序列决策中的信息收集与测试时探索。学生先在部分可观测条件下生成在策略历史，特权教师再依据该历史提供动作标签，从而避免离线模仿对不同隐状态动作取平均的问题。学生由此学会在不确定时主动搜索，并在隐状态显露后转向利用，其行为类似贝叶斯后验采样。实验覆盖离散探索、机器人操作、导航和Procgen等11类环境，在固定探索预算下取得约2倍回报、交互量较强化学习基线减少约100倍，并在视觉受限的真实机器人操作中相较标准蒸馏提升40%成功率。"
purpose = "- 为部分可观测任务提供比在线强化学习更节省交互数据的探索策略训练方案。\n- 表明即使特权教师本身无需探索，学生也能通过在策略历史条件化的动作监督习得信息收集行为。\n- 可用于机器人操作、导航等需要先辨识隐状态再执行任务的场景，并支持仿真到现实迁移。\n- 启示蒸馏系统应让教师标签匹配学生实际访问的历史分布，而非仅使用静态离线专家轨迹。"
notes = "标题：Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation Learns In-Context Exploration\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30105\n摘要：Sequential decision-making under partial observability is difficult because agents must explore to infer hidden, task-relevant information before acting effectively. Learning such information-gathering behaviors tabula rasa via online reinforcement learning is sample-inefficient, whereas offline imitation averages expert actions across hidden states and fails to learn exploration. In this work, we propose ASTEROID, a simple and effective student--teacher supervised learning framework that can learn test-time exploration. Although a privileged expert never needs to explore, we demonstrate that a history-conditioned student can learn to do so when trained with on-policy contexts: the student first collects on-policy context under partial observability, and the privileged expert provides acti"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "P2NPCBench通过多智能体对抗重写构造表述相近的P与NP完全问题配对基准，揭示代码模型的性能差距同时受到计算复杂度和教材式措辞熟悉度影响。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30103"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "P2NPCBench覆盖12个问题家族，将每个NP完全问题与仅有单一结构差异的多项式时间问题配对，以检验模型能否识别复杂度边界。AgentGAN采用生成器和冻结代码模型组成的对抗式多智能体框架，持续重写题目直至模型失败，并通过可执行有效性门控确保答案及复杂度类别保持不变。该基准最终包含2,114条强化后的题目陈述，并配有人工核验的参考求解器。对12个闭源及开源模型的评测显示，NP完全问题与P问题的pass@1分别为35.4%和63.8%；延长十倍时限仅带来有限改善，而对抗重写会将两类问题的准确率差距从24.8个百分点缩小至14.6个百分点。"
purpose = "- 用于区分模型是真正识别计算复杂度边界，还是依赖对教材式题目表述的记忆。\n- 可作为代码生成与算法推理系统的抗措辞变化评测基准。\n- 可执行有效性门控为自动生成答案保持、类别保持的高难度评测样本提供了参考。\n- 结果提示增加运行时间无法显著解决错误程序与程序崩溃，应优先改进算法选择和代码可靠性。"
notes = "标题：Did the Problem Actually Get Harder? Testing LLMs on Paired P and NP-Complete Problems Hardened by Adversarial Multi-Agent Rewriting\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30103\n摘要：Many NP-complete (NPC) problems have a polynomial-time (P) twin that differs in a single structural detail, such as 3- versus 2-colouring a graph. A model that reasons rather than recalls should notice which side of this boundary a problem falls on, yet existing benchmarks either fix the wording of each task or ignore its complexity class, so they cannot separate the two. We introduce P2NPCBench, a benchmark of 12 problem families in which every NPC problem is paired with a P twin posed over the same surface. To remove the textbook cues that enable recall, we build it with AgentGAN, an adversarial multi-agent framework in which a generator rewrites each statement until a panel of frozen code models fails, while an executable validity gate keeps every rewrite answer- and class-preserving. T"

summary = "该研究提出面向稀疏奖励特征的差分隐私 RLHF 理论与算法，在 Sample-DP 和 Label-DP 设置下改善了高维模型的隐私—效用权衡。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30099"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究聚焦 Private Sparse RLHF，假设人类偏好仅由 $s^* \\ll d$ 个相关特征决定，以避免私有奖励估计误差随模型维度多项式增长。作者在 Bradley-Terry-Luce 成对偏好模型下，为 Sample-DP 与 Label-DP 分别设计了高效的稀疏奖励估计算法。理论上，两种设置的平方 $\\ell_2$ 参数估计误差上界分别为 $\\widetilde{O}(s^*/n+{s^*}^2/(n^2\\varepsilon^2))$ 和 $\\widetilde{O}(s^*/n+s^*/(n\\varepsilon^2))$，并给出了相应下界与近最优性分析。情感生成和安全对齐实验表明，该框架相较稠密基线具有更优的隐私—效用权衡。"
purpose = "- 为使用敏感偏好数据训练奖励模型提供严格的差分隐私保障，降低用户信息泄露风险。\n- 利用奖励特征的稀疏性，使隐私估计误差主要依赖有效特征数而非庞大的模型维度。\n- 为私有 RLHF 算法的样本复杂度、误差上界及理论最优性提供分析基础。\n- 可用于隐私要求较高的内容偏好学习、情感生成与安全对齐场景。"
notes = "标题：Differentially Private Sparse Reward Estimation with Preference Feedback\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30099\n摘要：Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback (RLHF) has become a central paradigm for aligning Large Language Models with human values. However, the reliance on sensitive preference data necessitates rigorous differential privacy (DP) guarantees to prevent user information leakage. Existing private alignment methods typically assume dense reward parameterizations, resulting in estimation error bounds that scale polynomially with the ambient {model size $d$}. Such dependence is prohibitive in modern {overparameterized models} where $d$ is vast. In this work, we study \\emph{Private Sparse RLHF} problem to address this limitation, assuming that human preferences (reward model) are governed by a subset of $s^* \\ll d$ relevant features. We provide the first comprehensive theoretical framework for"

id = "atomicrl"
title = "AtomicRL：面向长程机器人操作的原子强化学习"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "AtomicRL利用原子任务结构改进VLA策略的信用分配与自适应探索，并通过Real2Sim2Real流程显著提升长程机器人操作性能。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30097"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "AtomicRL是一种面向长程机器人操作的强化学习后训练框架，将复杂任务拆分为原子子任务，以缓解轨迹级稀疏奖励导致的延迟和偏置信用分配。Semantic-Aware Policy Optimization（SAPO）通过语义感知门控阻止失败子任务的TD残差跨任务边界传播，同时保留子任务内部的信用分配与价值自举。Bottleneck-Adaptive Target Exploration（BATE）根据各原子子任务的掌握程度动态协调任务采样和局部探索，将训练集中于尚未掌握的瓶颈环节。实验中，该方法在LIBERO-LONG和CALVIN ABC-D上均优于PPO，并借助Real2Sim2Real流程将真实机器人任务表现平均提升21.3%。"
purpose = "- 为长程任务提供更细粒度的信用分配机制，减少失败信号在不同子任务之间的污染。\n- 可依据子任务掌握程度自适应调整采样与探索资源，提高强化学习后训练效率。\n- 展示从仿真在线强化学习迁移至真实机器人的可行路径，为VLA策略落地提供参考。"
notes = "标题：AtomicRL: Atomic Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30097\n摘要：Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown promise in robotic manipulation, but imitation learning (IL) alone often struggles to master complex long-horizon skills. Reinforcement learning (RL) offers an effective avenue for further improving VLA policies. However, existing VLA-RL methods typically suffer from delayed and biased credit assignment due to trajectory-level sparse rewards, while imbalanced subtask mastery creates bottlenecks that limit overall performance. To address these challenges, we propose AtomicRL, an RL post-training framework that leverages atomic task structures for reliable credit assignment and adaptive exploration. AtomicRL introduces Semantic-Aware Policy Optimization (SAPO), which uses semantic-aware gating to constrain TD residuals from failed atomic subtask"

summary = "该研究将并行智能体的编辑合并建模为带依赖图的离散块优化，通过验证增益、独立集与分数染色实现具备理论保证的安全协作。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30074"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "研究针对多个智能体各自提出有效编辑、合并后却可能破坏共享解的协作冲突，将其形式化为目标可精确评估且依赖关系已知的离散块优化问题。依赖图中的独立集用于识别兼容编辑，协调器可安全合并这些编辑，并额外评估存在交互的提案以捕获有利协同效应。基于最优分数染色的合并器可保留总验证局部改进的至少 $1/χ_f(G)$，且该界对仅从原提案中选择子集的合并策略是紧的；在局部改进与块误差界条件下，重复协作还能实现目标差距的几何收敛。代码修复、图割和路线搜索实验表明，该机制能够化解编辑冲突，并在任务结构、智能体分配、运行时间与词元成本之间提供可优化的权衡。"
purpose = "- 为并行代码修改、规划和组合优化智能体提供可验证的冲突感知合并机制，避免朴素合并抵消局部收益。\n- 可利用任务依赖图和分数染色，为并行协作的收益保留比例给出明确的最坏情况保证。\n- 将任务结构、智能体分配与计算成本解耦，可通过线性规划优化智能体数量及任务分派。\n- 提示多智能体系统不仅要评估单个提案，还应在同一当前解上统一验证增益并显式建模提案间交互。"
notes = "标题：Parallel AI Agent Collaboration as Graph-Structured Distributed Block Optimization\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30074\n摘要：Parallel AI agents can produce useful local edits yet degrade a shared solution when their edits are combined. We model this coordination problem as discrete block optimization with an exactly evaluable objective and a known dependency graph. Independent sets certify compatible edits whose verified gains, measured against the same current solution, add exactly. A coordinator constructs a safe compatible candidate and can also evaluate interacting edits to capture favorable interactions. We prove that an optimal fractional coloring yields a merger that preserves a $1/χ_f(G)$ fraction of the total verified local improvement, where $χ_f(G)$ is the graph's fractional chromatic number. This guarantee is tight among mergers that select a subset of the same proposals. Under conditional local-impr"

purpose = "- 说明图表可作为智能体推理循环中的认知动作，而不只是面向用户的最终展示形式。\n- 为数据分析智能体设计文本、数值与图表之间的动态工具路由策略提供依据。\n- 表明轨迹经验蒸馏结合验证门控可在不修改参数的情况下培养新的工具使用行为。\n- 提供评估智能体“具备工具读取能力”与“主动选择使用工具”之间差距的方法。"
notes = "标题：Charts as Epistemic Actions for Experience-Driven Agentic Data Analysis\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30072\n摘要：Data agents built on ReAct-style loops now execute complex analytical workflows over structured data, yet their intermediate reasoning still runs almost entirely through text. Human analysts instead move between numbers, tables, and charts, reading visual evidence to expose trends and anomalies that text summaries drop. We ask whether charts can serve as internal epistemic actions inside a data agent's reasoning loop, and how to teach an agent to use them. We first build \\probe{}, a suite of 200 controlled tasks under four matched interface conditions, and find a persistent gap between what agents can read and what they choose to use. Agents score as well from self-generated charts as from numeric output, yet plotting access alone does not translate into consistent use. Task slices show th"

notes = "标题：GoiBERT: Compact Vietnamese Language Models via Two-Stage Knowledge Distillation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30067\n摘要：Vietnamese pre-trained encoders have improved rapidly, but the strongest ones are large, multilingual-vocabulary models or depend on external word segmentation, which makes them costly to deploy. A same-tokenizer Vietnamese encoder first teaches general-purpose representations. A much larger teacher then provides task-level guidance through soft labels that are generated without ever labeling its own training data. Across eight benchmarks covering text classification, aspect-based sentiment analysis, natural language inference, and named entity recognition, GoiBERT-large achieves the highest average score among non-te"

summary = "通过在每个去噪步骤中循环执行部分早期 Transformer 层，循环式 UDLM 在不改变训练目标与采样器的情况下显著降低训练 FLOPs，并改善生成困惑度和推理效率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30066"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "均匀扩散语言模型（UDLM）需要从可能不可靠的上下文中迭代去噪，训练成本高于始终基于干净上下文的掩码扩散语言模型。该工作在保持训练目标和采样器不变的前提下，于每个去噪步骤中递归执行少量早期 Transformer 层。循环式 UDLM 在 OpenWebText 和 FineWeb-Edu 上分别以最高 2.8 倍和 3.3 倍更少的训练 FLOPs，达到非循环模型训练约 0.5 万亿词元后的验证 NLL。在相同函数评估次数（NFE）预算下，其生成困惑度最高降低 9.8%；训练时提高较小循环次数的采样频率，还能在下游准确率不降的情况下减少 12% 的推理循环。"
purpose = "- 说明循环复用 Transformer 层能够缓解 UDLM 训练目标带来的高计算成本。\n- 为固定 FLOP 或 NFE 预算下的扩散语言模型提供训练与推理加速方案。\n- 表明循环次数分布本身是重要的效率控制变量，可通过偏向较小循环次数改善部署效率。\n- 为参数共享、循环计算与扩散式文本生成的结合提供实证依据。"
notes = "标题：Looped Uniform Diffusion Language Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30066\n摘要：Uniform diffusion language models (UDLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising sequences of ordinary vocabulary tokens, any of which may be corrupted and subsequently revised. During training, UDLMs must denoise from a context that may itself be unreliable, whereas masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) always condition on a clean context. This challenging training objective makes UDLMs more FLOP-intensive to train than MDLMs, but it yields stronger downstream reasoning performance. Recently, selective looping, which recursively applies a few early Transformer layers within each denoising step, has been shown to improve training efficiency in MDLMs under an iso-FLOP budget. We investigate looping in UDLMs while keeping the training objective and sampler unchanged. We find that looping "

notes = "标题：SP-HLM: Sparse-Pose-Driven Motion Generation and Whole-Body Control for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30044\n摘要：Humanoid loco-manipulation requires an effective interface between high-level task policies and whole-body control. SP-VLA, a vision-language-action model, first predicts task-relevant sparse poses and finger joint positions from multimodal observations and language instructions using asynchronous action-chunk inference. Given these sparse targets, SP-MGM, a motion"

content = "研究发现，文本技能的有效作用高度集中于少量轨迹位置，前 10% 的位置可覆盖 96.12% 的正向技能增益。Behavioral Localization 对比冻结智能体在有无文本技能时的输出，筛选既提高成功词元概率、又显著改变下一词元分布的位置。随后仅使用这些局部位置监督短软提示，避免完整成功轨迹引入不必要的行为变化。在 SpreadsheetBench 上，共享提示设置的成功率由完整轨迹监督的 30.36% 提升至 36.79%；任务特定设置达到 57.38%，超过完整文本技能与在策略 OPCD 基线。"

notes = "标题：Robust Soft Skill Adaptation via Behavioral Localization in Tool-Using Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30041\n摘要：Text skills can extend the capabilities of tool-using agents, but long skill descriptions increase computational costs. Soft prompts provide a compact alternative, yet learning from entire successful trajectories may also introduce unnecessary behavioral changes. We find that the useful effect of a text skill is highly concentrated in a small subset of trajectory positions. Based on this observation, we propose Behavioral Localization, which compares the frozen agent with and without the text skill to identify positions where the skill both increases the likelihood of successful tokens and meaningfully changes the next token distribution. We use these localized positions to supervise a short soft prompt. Experiments on SpreadsheetBench with a frozen Qwen3.6-35B-A3B agent show that the top "

summary = "研究发现主流文档转换器会丢失颜色与高亮等关键格式语义，导致企业智能体在政策要求识别中自信地产生错误。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30026"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究基于 76 份真实政策文档构建任务，要求智能体恢复作者通过格式特别标出的要求，并使用 OOXML 属性和确定性匹配器评分，无需 LLM 评判。忽略格式后，micro F1 从 0.690 降至 Docling 的 0.150 和纯文本的 0.075，这一现象在来自两家厂商的六个回答模型上均可复现。留一消融表明，性能损失主要来自高亮和颜色线索，而粗体、斜体、等宽字体及删除线几乎没有影响；PDF 中也存在相同的不对称性。对 6,460 份文档的统计显示，32.8% 含有刻意使用的颜色或高亮，10.3% 将其用于义务条款；显式标注线索类型还可在保留格式的基础上进一步提升表现。"
purpose = "- 企业智能体的可靠性瓶颈可能位于文档摄取层，而非后续推理模型，应将格式语义保真纳入端到端评估。\n- Word、PDF 解析管线应显式保留颜色和高亮，并将其转换为智能体可识别的结构化标记。\n- 文档解析基准应增加格式线索恢复、误报率及跨格式一致性指标，避免只评估纯文本提取质量。\n- OOXML 属性可作为低成本、确定性的监督信号，用于构建无需 LLM 裁判的文档理解评测。"
notes = "标题：What the Author Wrote, What the Agent Reads: Silent Cue Loss in Enterprise Document Ingestion\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30026\n摘要：Enterprise agents act on documents. Before an agent can follow a policy, answer a compliance question or file a ticket, some ingestion layer must turn a Word file or PDF into tokens - and that layer is where an agent deployed in the wild silently loses information. We show that one layer is lost by every mainstream converter - the meaning an author encodes in how text looks - and that the loss makes agents confidently wrong rather than visibly incomplete. We build a task from 76 real policy documents: recover the requirements the author singled out, scored against the file's own OOXML properties by a deterministic matcher with no LLM judge. Formatting-blind ingestion collapses from micro F1 0.690 to 0.150 (docling) and 0.075 (plain text), a gap that replicates across six answering models f"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30014"
code = ""

content = "- 该工作指出，任务成功率相近的 LLM 智能体仍可能采取截然不同的动作序列，因此仅凭最终成绩不足以描述智能体行为。\n- Agent Behavior Map 使用共享参考轨迹上的中心化动作对数似然，为 LLM—脚手架组合构建行为坐标，无需让目标智能体产生新的环境交互轨迹。\n- 理论上，作者在明确假设下给出了坐标平方距离与轨迹 KL 散度之间的误差界；在八个交叉评分模型中，该距离与实测轨迹 KL 的排序一致。\n- 在覆盖 100 个 LLM 和四种环境的实验中，基于 ALFWorld 的坐标能够预测性能并发现跨模型家族的行为相似性，且这种相似性在控制成功率和模型规模后依然存在。\n- 扩展实验显示，不同脚手架组件产生的行为位移可近似组合，但组合精度会随组件及环境变化。"
purpose = "- 为智能体提供区别于成功率的行为级表征，揭示性能相近系统之间的策略差异。\n- 可在不执行昂贵环境 rollout 的前提下进行智能体筛选、聚类和性能预测，降低评估成本。\n- 有助于分析不同 LLM 与脚手架组件对行为的独立及组合影响，为脚手架设计和组件替换提供量化依据。\n- 可用于发现跨模型家族的行为等价关系，并辅助构建更具行为多样性的评测集合。"
notes = "标题：Agent Behavior Maps: Locating Agents by What They Would Do, Without Running Them\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30014\n摘要：LLM agents with similar success rates can follow different action sequences. An Agent Behavior Map represents LLM-harness combinations using centered action log-likelihoods on shared reference trajectories, without requiring new environment rollouts from the target agent. We derive error bounds relating squared coordinate distances to trajectory KL under explicit assumptions, and across eight cross-scored models the distances track the directly measured trajectory KL in rank order. Across 100 LLMs and four environments, ALFWorld-based coordinates support performance prediction and identify behavioral similarities. In ALFWorld, these similarities persist after controlling for success rates and model size, including across model families. As an extension, harness-component displacements appr"

summary = "TIDE通过行为驱动的决策树归纳，将基准测试结果组织为层次化能力树，从而揭示聚合分数无法呈现的模型细粒度能力差异。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29985"
code = ""

content = "TIDE将诊断评测重新表述为基于模型行为的决策树归纳，从基准样本及其模型得分中递归构建层次化能力树。每个树节点由LLM评判模型提出候选划分并完成样本分组，再利用行为信息增益选择最具诊断价值的划分。作者在四个基准、五个模型上验证该方法，发现聚合分数掩盖了不同模型在微观经济市场推理、合同法推理等具体能力上的显著差异。该框架可扩展至既有和未来基准，以更充分地利用评测结果中的行为信号。"
purpose = "- 将单一聚合分数转化为可解释的层次化能力诊断，帮助定位模型优势与短板。\n- 可用于比较不同模型在具体任务类别上的能力差异，为模型选择、训练和迭代提供依据。\n- 为既有基准提供低成本的二次分析方式，无需重新设计完整评测体系。\n- 提示评测实践应同时报告总体指标与细粒度行为结构，避免聚合指标掩盖关键能力差异。"
notes = "标题：TIDE: Tree-Induced Fine-Grained Diagnostic Evaluation from Model Behaviors\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29985\n摘要：Benchmark performance is the dominant standard for comparing large language models (LLMs), typically summarized by a single aggregate score, such as accuracy, pass rate, or rubric score. This aggregate score provides limited information about a model's underlying capabilities, yet understanding these capabilities is crucial for model development and evaluation. To this end, we propose Tree-Induced Diagnostic Evaluation (TIDE), which recasts diagnostic evaluation as behavior-driven decision-tree induction to perform fine-grained capability diagnosis. TIDE recursively builds a hierarchical capability tree from benchmark samples and model scores: at each node, an LLM judge proposes candidate splits and performs sample-to-group classification, and behavior-based information gain selects among "

content = "- FaultGene 接收基线 Scenic/CARLA 场景与结构化故障规格，生成能够体现硬件在环故障注入影响的可执行测试用例。\n- 框架通过语义一致性检查，确保生成结果保留原始驾驶场景的预期上下文。\n- 离线改进阶段结合执行级反馈，修正未产生预期故障退化效果的候选测试，并构建高质量微调监督数据。\n- 微调后的生成器可在部署时直接生成故障增强测试，无需人工设计参考执行或反复运行改进循环。"
purpose = "- 将故障场景准备从人工流程转为自动生成，有助于扩大自动驾驶硬件在环测试的覆盖范围。\n- 结合语义检查与真实执行反馈，可提高生成测试的可执行性和故障触发有效性。\n- 该框架为传感器故障、嵌入式计算故障及其传播效应提供了可复现、可扩展的验证路径。"
notes = "标题：FaultGene: LLM-Assisted Fault-Augmented Test Generation for Hardware-in-the-Loop Validation of Autonomous Driving Systems\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29969\n摘要：Reliable validation of autonomous driving cyber-physical systems requires testing not only nominal driving behavior but also failures arising from faults in sensing and embedded computation. Physical road testing provides realistic system behavior but is costly and difficult to reproduce, while simulation enables scalable evaluation but cannot fully capture hardware-dependent behavior and fault propagation. Hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) validation bridges this gap by executing the driving stack on real embedded hardware within a simulated environment. However, preparing fault-augmented scenarios for HIL testing remains largely a manual process, limiting test coverage, scalability, and efficiency. We present FaultGene, an LLM-assisted framework for automated generation of executable fault-augm"


[[items]]
id = "cord"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "CORD通过检测并投影相互冲突的强化学习与自蒸馏梯度，减少目标抵消并提升智能体任务成功率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29965"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "CORD面向强化学习任务奖励与自蒸馏教师指导联合训练时的梯度冲突问题，避免仅靠混合系数调节两个目标的相对尺度。框架将受保护目标的选择与触发梯度投影的条件解耦，并在每个优化器小批次中仅执行一次梯度组合。其最近点形式可导出闭式更新，并刻画优化器变换前更新对受保护目标的一阶影响。CORD可直接接入不同的联合训练方案，在ALFWorld上将成功率提高1.5至15.9个百分点，并在WebShop的部分配置中将SDAR成功率从60.7%提升至64.6%。"
purpose = "- 为联合强化学习与自蒸馏提供可插拔的梯度冲突修正机制。\n- 表明保护方向与投影触发条件需要联合设计，而不能独立选择。\n- 可通过梯度诊断识别目标抵消造成的局部优化进展损失。\n- 提示投影后蒸馏目标及其混合权重仍会显著影响训练效果。"
notes = "标题：CORD: Conflict-Aware Optimization for Joint RL and Distillation in Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29965\n摘要：Joint reinforcement learning and self-distillation combines task rewards with dense teacher guidance for agent training, but their gradients can partly cancel. A mixing coefficient controls relative scale without selectively removing opposing components. We present CORD, a general framework for conflict-aware optimization of two objectives. CORD separates the choice of protected objective from the condition that triggers projection, and combines the gradients once per optimizer mini-batch. A nearest-point formulation yields a closed-form update and characterizes its first-order effect on the protected objective before optimizer transformations. Gradient diagnostics connect conflict in agent training to lost local optimization progress and motivate selective correction. As a gradient-combin"


[[items]]
id = "tom2vec"
title = "ToM2Vec：用于多智能体协调的连续心智理论词元"

score = 2

summary = "ToM2Vec通过固定预算的连续推理词元在潜空间中表征伙伴的信念、欲望和潜在行动，从而提升多种协作、竞争及混合动机场景中的多智能体协调能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29934"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "ToM2Vec使用固定数量的连续推理词元，在潜空间中构建抽象的心智理论表示，以容纳伙伴状态的不确定性和多种可能性。训练时，这些词元由伙伴的真实思维轨迹监督，并学习对其进行抽象与匹配；测试时则由智能体自行生成，用于调节后续思考和行动。该方法在Overcooked-AI、多方谈判、Decrypto、Hanabi及Collab Escape/Capture等环境中稳定优于基础模型和多种训练或提示基线。实验还表明，将潜在推理改为文本表达会导致性能下降，且该方法能够泛化到训练阶段未见过的伙伴模型家族，包括GPT-5.4。"
purpose = "- 为多智能体系统提供比自然语言描述更紧凑、可表达不确定性的伙伴心理状态表征。\n- 表明连续潜空间词元可作为智能体思考与行动的条件信号，改善协作、竞争和混合动机任务中的协调表现。\n- 为跨模型伙伴泛化提供训练思路，使智能体能够适应训练时未见的模型家族。\n- 可通过信念、欲望和下一步行动问答检验潜在表示是否真正编码心智理论，而非仅记忆任务信息。"
notes = "标题：ToM2Vec: Continuous Theory-of-Mind Tokens for Multi-Agent Coordination\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29934\n摘要：Forming an accurate theory-of-mind (ToM) is key to successful multi-agent collaboration and requires reasoning under uncertainty to capture multiple possible partner states. This is hard to do in the text representation space native to large language model (LLM) agents, which more easily describe a single partner state. To address this challenge, we introduce ToM2Vec, which learns to form abstract theories-of-mind in a rich latent space via a fixed budget of continuous reasoning tokens. During training, supervision for these tokens comes from the partner's actual thoughts, which the ToM tokens are trained to abstract and match. At test-time, ToM tokens are generated by each agent and act as conditioners for that agent's thoughts and actions. Across several environments covering collaborati"

summary = "论文将评测基准形式化为难度轴上的经验测度，诊断难度分布失配与覆盖缺口，并通过 BenchFill 定向改写题目以改善局部覆盖。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29927"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出基于测度的基准评估视角，将测试题集合表示为难度轴上的经验分布。作者形式化了两类核心失效模式：基准与目标难度分布之间的测度失配，以及无法观测局部训练进展的难度覆盖缺口。对横跨 7 个领域的 25 个流行基准进行分析后发现，不少基准会隐式过度加权特定难度区间，或遗漏大段难度范围。论文进一步提出 BenchFill，通过面向目标难度的题目改写来缩小局部覆盖缺口。"
purpose = "- 为基准设计和审计提供难度分布层面的量化框架，避免仅凭平均分或排行榜判断模型能力。\n- 帮助识别基准对特定难度区间的偏重及覆盖盲区，提高评估结论的测量效度。\n- 可利用 BenchFill 定向补充缺失难度区间，使基准更敏感地追踪模型训练过程中的局部进步。"
notes = "标题：Your Benchmark Is an Empirical Measure Over Difficulty\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29927\n摘要：A benchmark is not merely a collection of test questions; it naturally induces an empirical measure over difficulty. In this work, we develop this measure-based view of benchmark evaluation. This perspective allows us to identify and formalize two central failure modes: measure mismatch, where the benchmark difficulty distribution differs from the intended difficulty distribution, and coverage gaps, where uncovered regions along the difficulty axis prevent benchmarks from detecting meaningful localized training progress. We propose two diagnostic metrics that capture these two failure modes and provide rigorous theoretical guarantees. Empirically, we analyze 25 popular benchmarks spanning 7 domains using carefully estimated item difficulties. Our results show that many benchmarks implicitl"

summary = "针对特定人类价值维度的微调会外溢影响未训练价值、风险偏好、社会认知与安全特征，表明价值对齐需要更全面的行为评估。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29922"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究在四个指令微调 LLM 上分别针对 Hofstede 文化价值维度进行适配，并考察跨价值迁移及更广泛的行为变化。实验发现，单一价值维度的训练不仅改变目标价值，也会影响未经训练的其他价值。此类训练还可能改变模型的风险偏好、社会认知能力和安全特征，产生难以预料的行为副作用。作者因此主张在后训练价值对齐中引入覆盖更广的行为评估，并重新审视价值训练在整个训练流水线中的最佳位置。"
purpose = "- 提醒价值对齐实践不能只检查目标价值指标，还应系统评估非目标行为与安全属性。\n- 可用于设计覆盖跨价值迁移、风险偏好和社会认知的后训练回归测试。\n- 启示开发者比较不同训练阶段实施价值注入的效果，降低价值微调引发的能力与行为漂移。"
notes = "标题：Unintended Side Effects of Value Training in LLMs\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29922\n摘要：As LLMs are increasingly deployed in value-sensitive contexts, aligning them with human values has emerged as a post-training objective in NLP, aimed at instilling additional values and guiding model behaviour. However, current evaluations remain narrow. Because values are interconnected, it is unclear how fine-tuning for specific values alters unrelated capabilities and behaviours. In this work, we adapt four instruction-tuned LLMs across individual Hofstede's value dimensions and evaluate them on both cross-value transfer and broader behaviours. We find that value-specific training produces unintended side effects on untrained values, risk preferences, social cognition, and safety traits. Consequently, we suggest that value alignment after post-training should incorporate broader behavio"

summary = "研究评估 Gemini Flash 对低分辨率热成像视频进行零样本跌倒筛查的能力，显示其误报率较低，但召回率显著受分辨率和提示措辞影响。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29913"
code = ""

content = "研究将 Gemini 3.8 Flash 和 Gemini 3.7 Flash 作为事件触发式跌倒筛查器的决策模块，直接处理短热成像视频及可用音轨，无需任务专用训练。在公开的 32×24 热成像数据集上，部署提示对留出受试者跌倒的检出率为 69%，且在 499 个非跌倒窗口中没有误报。删除提示中关于“离开视野不算跌倒”的警示语后，Gemini 3.8 Flash 的灵敏度升至 83%，家庭场景中可见跌倒的检出数由 4/9 升至 9/9，但会把主动躺下误判为跌倒。模型在 8×6 分辨率下灵敏度降至 34%，重复请求有 4% 的结果不一致；传统监督检测器虽在实验室数据上召回率更高，却在真实家庭环境中产生每日 14.2 至 23.9 次误报。"
purpose = "- 展示多模态 LLM 在隐私友好的居家老人跌倒监测中充当零样本决策器的可行性。\n- 提醒实际部署必须系统评估提示措辞、输入分辨率和重复推理的不稳定性。\n- 表明实验室内高召回率不足以代表真实家庭场景表现，误报率与跨环境泛化应作为核心指标。\n- 公开逐帧跌倒标注和模型回答，可用于后续提示优化、模型比较与真实世界评测。"
notes = "标题：Asking a Multimodal LLM Whether Someone Fell: Zero-Shot Screening of Low-Resolution Thermal Video in a Laboratory and an Occupied Home\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29913\n摘要：Thermal cameras can watch over older people living alone without capturing faces, and multimodal large language models (LLMs) can judge a short video without task-specific training. We evaluate a production multimodal LLM, Gemini 3.8 Flash at temperature 0, as the decision stage of an event-triggered fall screener, used exactly as a deployed prototype uses it: one request per short thermal clip, with its sound track when there is one, and its predecessor Gemini 3.7 Flash on the same requests. On 438 staged falls and 563 non-fall windows that we located by hand in a public 32x24 thermal dataset, the deployed request called 69% of the held-out subjects' falls (67% over all subjects; bed falls excluded) and none of 499 non-fall windows, including people lying on the floor and getting up. Over"

summary = "PAUSE在编程智能体首次修改源码前注入一次独立规划，以较低额外成本提升SWE-bench任务修复成功率并减少失败任务恢复所需的执行开销。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29870"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "PAUSE观察到，编程智能体通常在轨迹完成约60%时才首次编辑源码，此前已进行了仓库探索、缺陷复现和测试。框架在首次编辑请求执行前暂停智能体，由独立Planner读取任务与编辑前轨迹并生成一次修复计划，随后原Executor保留上下文和工作区继续执行。该机制不需要学习型监控器、检查点搜索或额外的Executor调用，并同时支持在线运行和基于历史轨迹前缀的离线恢复。在SWE-bench Verified与Multilingual上，PAUSE最高带来16.8%的解决率提升，且离线恢复可挽救35.4%的合格失败任务，并比重新启动节省33%至41%的Executor词元。"
purpose = "- 将首次源码编辑作为稳定且低成本的干预点，避免频繁审查完整执行轨迹。\n- 通过独立Planner与原Executor分工，在保留工作区状态的同时纠正程序修复方向。\n- 可用于在线提升编程智能体成功率，也可从失败轨迹前缀恢复执行并降低词元成本。\n- 为智能体运行时干预提供无需异常检测器和检查点搜索的简化方案。"
notes = "标题：PAUSE: Search-Free Steering of Coding Agents Before Their First Source-Code Edit\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29870\n摘要：Coding agents can invest substantial effort in repository exploration and testing yet still produce unsuccessful patches. Existing online steering methods review trajectories periodically or invoke an advisor when a monitor detects behavioral problems; frequent reviews are costly, and the absence of anomalous behavior does not mean that an agent is on the right track. We propose PAUSE, a framework for steering coding agents with a single planning intervention. Agents reach their first source-code edit at a median of 60% of trajectory progress, after exploration, bug reproduction, and testing. PAUSE intercepts this edit request before it executes, which requires no learned monitor, no search over checkpoints, and no extra model call (search-free). A separate Planner reads the task and the p"

summary = "论文将机器人零样本泛化建模为对部署分布的覆盖问题，并提出通过贡献者多样性、共享动作潜空间和生成式增强提升未见任务与环境中的成功率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29829"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文指出，通用机器人需要应对训练阶段未见的任务、物体和环境，但当前机器人数据采集集中于少数团队，数据量增加并不等同于分布多样性提升。作者将零样本泛化表述为对部署分布的覆盖，并论证重复增加相同采集者的样本只能缓慢缩小未知区域，而引入新贡献者更可能扩展覆盖范围。为此，论文提出机器人动作的共享潜空间，结合多模态对齐、生成式数据增强与噪声标签学习。作者还建议围绕标准化且易用的 Origami 机器人建立开发者社区，并给出用于检验数据贡献多样性与真实机器人零样本成功率关系的评估协议。"
purpose = "- 为机器人数据采集提供从追求样本规模转向扩大贡献者与场景多样性的设计依据。\n- 可利用共享动作潜空间统一不同应用、任务和贡献者产生的异构机器人数据。\n- 为评估具身系统的零样本泛化建立可检验假设，将数据多样性与真实机器人成功率直接关联。\n- 启发通过社区化数据贡献和标准机器人平台降低具身数据采集的集中化程度。"
notes = "标题：Robot Generalization Beyond Volume: The Diversity Path via Zero-Shot Capabilities\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29829\n摘要：General-purpose robots must handle tasks, objects and environments they were never trained on, yet zero-shot generalization remains one of the least solved problems in physical AI. In digital AI, zero-shot capability grew with the volume and, above all, the diversity of training data, much of it sourced from the crowd. Robotics instead relies on paid collection by a few well-funded teams, which yields volume without diversity. We formulate zero-shot generalization in physical AI as coverage of the deployment distribution, and show that more samples from the same collectors shrink the unseen region only slowly, while each new contributor can extend it. Building on this and on zero-shot learning, we propose a shared latent space for robot actions that combines multi-modal alignment, generati"

id = "userswe"
title = "UserSWE：动态用户多轮对话中的编程智能体评测"

]
score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "UserSWE 通过动态模拟用户、多轮信息披露和逐轮执行验证，评估编程智能体修复代码仓库级缺陷的真实交互能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29822"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "UserSWE 将代码仓库历史和现有单轮基准转换为多轮缺陷修复任务，以弥补传统基准一次性提供完整问题描述的局限。每轮对话中，信息池约束模拟用户可披露的内容，基于 20,859 次开发会话校准的角色转移模型则控制用户的表达方式。系统在每轮后运行测试，并由来自不同模型家族的两个智能体结合测试证据生成和审核判定，从而减少错误接受或拒绝补丁的情况。作者在来自 42 个代码仓库的 72 项任务上评测了 11 个智能体，发现智能体排名会随交互轮次预算显著变化，说明单轮成绩不足以刻画交互式编程能力。"
purpose = "- 为编程智能体提供更贴近真实开发流程的多轮缺陷修复评测框架。\n- 通过逐轮测试和双智能体判定提高补丁验收结果的可靠性。\n- 揭示交互预算对智能体排名的影响，提示评测报告应同时展示不同轮次下的性能。\n- 可用于研究动态用户行为、渐进式需求披露及智能体澄清与修复能力。"
notes = "标题：UserSWE: Evaluating Coding Agents in Multi-Turn Conversations with Dynamic Users\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29822\n摘要：As coding agents become more capable, developers increasingly use them to fix bugs through multi-turn interactions. Existing benchmarks do not adequately cover this scenario. Most present a complete issue report in a single turn without interaction. Their evaluation can produce both false positives and false negatives, accepting incorrect patches or rejecting correct ones. We introduce UserSWE for evaluating coding agents' ability to fix repository-level bugs through multi-turn interactions with dynamic users and execution-grounded verdicts at every turn. Its construction pipeline turns repository history and existing single-turn benchmarks into multi-turn tasks. At each turn, information pools constrain what the simulated user can reveal, while a persona chain sampled from a transition mo"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "COMET通过协调信号完整性、物理一致性与能力边界三层监控，将异常检测结果转化为面向人类监督者的故障诊断，减少安全关键AI系统在分布偏移和传感器故障下的静默错误。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29816"
code = ""
dataset = ""

content = "COMET为人类监督的AI安全系统设计了多层运行时监控架构，每个监控器分别锚定一种故障模式。框架包含信号完整性、物理一致性和AI能力边界三层，并通过协调层融合输出，再以交通灯界面向监督者传达诊断信息。作者在压缩空气泄漏开放数据上模拟协变量偏移、语义偏移和传感器故障，所有监控器冻结后进行零样本测试。COMET在协变量偏移的无泄漏数据及四类传感器故障中的三类上，将可信错误回答率降至零，同时保持约1%的标称误报率；语义偏移下的残余漏检则揭示了被动监控的能力边界。"
purpose = "- 为安全关键AI系统提供可解释、可操作的运行时故障检测与隔离方案。\n- 说明多个针对不同故障模式的监控器应通过协调层组合，而非仅依赖单一异常检测器。\n- 将匿名告警映射为面向人类监督者的定性诊断，有助于提升人在回路决策效率。\n- 提醒系统设计者：被动监控难以完全覆盖语义偏移，仍需结合主动验证、故障恢复或安全降级机制。"
notes = "标题：COMET: A coordinated multi-layer runtime monitoring framework for human-supervised AI-enabled safety systems\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29816\n摘要：Incorporating AI systems into safety systems requires monitoring to avoid silent faults and ensure safe and reliable operation. Single monitors often do not provide actionable information for human supervisors, who act as the system's adaptive controller. We present COMET, a coordinated multi-layer runtime monitoring framework for human-supervised AI-enabled safety systems in which each monitor is anchored in a different fault mode. Monitor outputs are combined in a coordination layer and mapped by a communication layer to a traffic-light display. In this work, COMET consists of three monitoring layers: signal integrity, physics consistency, and the AI system's competence envelope. The framework is evaluated on an open dataset of compressed-air leaks, where an AI system detects leaks from "

summary = "论文提出操作级测量框架，通过对比基础能力画像与交互轨迹中的操作表现，定位大模型从基础能力到智能体执行能力的实现差距。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29751"
code = ""

content = "论文分别基于能力基准和交互轨迹构建模型的基础能力画像与操作画像。框架将实际操作表现相对基础能力预测值的偏差定义为“能力实现差距”，用于区分基础能力不足与部署执行薄弱。对 τ²-Bench 中 15 个模型服务的实验表明，基础能力只能部分预测智能体操作表现，基础画像相近的模型在执行层面仍可能存在显著差异。引入操作画像后，任务分数预测效果明显改善，且所识别的执行差距在不同任务子集间保持一致。"
purpose = "- 为智能体失败提供操作级诊断，避免仅凭端到端分数混淆基础能力不足与执行部署问题。\n- 可用于比较基础能力相近模型的工具使用、状态处理和任务执行差异。\n- 为模型开发和智能体评估提供更具可操作性的能力实现差距指标。\n- 说明评测智能体时应联合分析静态能力基准与真实交互轨迹。"
notes = "标题：Bridging Foundational and Agentic Capabilities of LLMs\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29751\n摘要：Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as agents, yet it remains unclear how their foundational capabilities translate into agentic capabilities. End-to-end scores leave unresolved whether low benchmark capability or weak deployment drives a failure, limiting actionable diagnosis. We bridge foundational and agentic capabilities through an operation-level measurement framework. We represent each model with a foundational profile derived from capability benchmarks and an operation profile derived from interaction trajectories, and define capability realization gaps as deviations of observed operation performance from predictions based on foundational capabilities. Experiments on fifteen model services in $τ^2$-Bench show that foundational profiles only partially predict agent"

score = 2

summary = "研究提出基于思维链与输出互信息最大化的无监督后训练目标，可恢复受损的思维链可监控性并增强对不安全行为的预测能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29734"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究指出，思维链只有与模型实际输出和行为保持耦合，才能成为有效的安全监控信号，而思维链压缩、针对监控器的优化及合成文档引发的上下文外泛化都可能破坏这种耦合。作者提出一种无监督后训练目标，通过最大化思维链与最终输出之间的互信息来提升可监控性。在三个思维链可监控性退化的模型实验体上，该方法恢复了大部分或全部损失。用于未退化策略时，它还能提升人工评判的可监控性、思维链监控器及激活探针对不安全行为的预测效果，并增加具有行为后果的推理内容。"
purpose = "- 将可监控性视为可直接训练和优化的策略属性，而非模型训练后的固定副产品。\n- 可在常规后训练流程中加入独立的可监控性阶段，补充仅避免直接优化思维链的被动保护措施。\n- 为检测危险推理、奖励作弊或规避监控行为提供更可靠的思维链与内部激活信号。\n- 提示安全训练需持续检验思维链、最终输出与真实行为之间的耦合程度。"
notes = "标题：Monitorability Is Trainable: Post-Training for Chain-of-Thought Monitorability\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29734\n摘要：Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is one of the few practical tools available for overseeing capable AI systems, but it is fragile: a model's reasoning is only useful to a monitor if it remains coupled to what the model actually does. This coupling can be degraded during training through, for example, CoT compression, optimization against a monitor, and out-of-context generalization from synthetic documents. In this work, we propose an unsupervised post-training objective based on maximizing mutual information between CoT and the output for improving CoT monitorability. Across three model organisms with degraded CoT, it recovers most or all of the lost monitorability. Applied to undegraded policies, it further improves judged monitorability, improves prediction of unsafe behavior by CoT mo"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "该研究为单个视觉-语言-动作策略添加无需梯度训练的核方差头，以低成本估计认知不确定性并支持示范获取和求助决策。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29730"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究提出事后核方差头，利用单个 VLA 策略的嵌入以及训练示范计算高斯过程条件方差，从而估计认知不确定性。该组件不需要回归目标或梯度更新，也不会改变原有策略。在 SmolVLA 与 LIBERO-10 实验中，其任务成功相关性、主动学习示范获取和向更强监督策略转介的表现可媲美双策略集成，同时将部分不确定性评分耗时最多降低约 50 倍。研究还指出现有评测偏重任务间覆盖差异，并通过受控扰动验证不同位置的方差头对陌生模态输入及陌生跨模态组合的识别能力。"
purpose = "- 为部署中的 VLA 策略提供低成本、即插即用的认知不确定性估计，无需训练和运行多个策略。\n- 可根据不确定性触发示范请求、主动学习或向人类及更强监督策略转介，降低高风险场景中的失败成本。\n- 提示具身策略的不确定性评测应同时考察任务间覆盖识别与任务内样本排序，避免执行随机性掩盖实际能力。\n- 模态专用头与融合头可分别用于检测单一输入分布偏移和跨模态组合异常。"
notes = "标题：Post-hoc Epistemic Uncertainty for Vision-Language-Action Policies\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29730\n摘要：Vision-language-action (VLA) policies need to recognize gaps in their training knowledge to request useful demonstrations or assistance in new situations, especially when failures are costly. Disagreement within an ensemble of independently trained policies can guide these decisions, but requires training and running multiple policies. We propose post-hoc kernel-variance heads that estimate epistemic uncertainty from the embeddings of a single policy. Each head computes the conditional variance of a Gaussian process conditioned on embeddings from the training demonstrations. Fitting requires no regression targets or gradient updates and leaves the policy unchanged. In experiments with SmolVLA on LIBERO-10, the kernel variance heads perform competitively with two-policy ensemble scores in t"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "LASAGNA 对八项基准上的 450 种 RAG 配置进行受控评测，发现组件的孤立增益难以直接叠加，且最优组合会随评测目标和语料规模变化。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29722"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "作者提出开源框架 LASAGNA，用于受控评估嵌入、分块、检索、重排、查询增强、上下文压缩和智能体验证等 RAG 组件的组合效果。实验覆盖八项基准、450 种配置以及包含 5,000 至 20 万篇文档的不同规模语料库。结果显示，三个检索组件单独测得的增益预计可叠加至 47%，实际联合使用时却仅提升 17%。检索质量与答案忠实度对组件组合的偏好并不一致，优化其中一项甚至可能损害另一项；同时，小规模语料上的最佳配置在语料扩大后未必仍然最优。"
purpose = "- 评估新 RAG 技术时，应将其置于完整流水线中测试，避免以单组件实验推断实际系统收益。\n- 应分别报告检索质量与答案忠实度，识别两类目标之间的权衡关系。\n- RAG 配置选择需要考虑语料库规模，不宜将小规模实验结论直接迁移至生产环境。\n- LASAGNA 可用于开展组件消融、组合搜索和跨规模配置验证。"
notes = "标题：Retrieval Augmented Generation Compositionality\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29722\n摘要：Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) grounds large language models in external knowledge. Modern pipelines combine embedding, chunking, retrieval, reranking, query augmentation, context compression, and agentic verification, yet these techniques are typically evaluated one at a time. It remains unclear whether their isolated gains survive composition, whether retrieval quality and answer faithfulness favor the same design choices, and whether conclusions from small corpora hold at large scale. We pre-declare three hypotheses and introduce LASAGNA, an open-source framework for controlled RAG evaluation. We test 450 configurations across eight benchmarks and corpora ranging from 5K to 200K documents. Three findings emerge. First, individually strong techniques from different phases of a RAG "

content = "- 作者沿语言数量、文字系统数量和模型容量三个维度训练了一系列 MPIXEL 像素语言模型，并保持每种语言的计算量固定。\n- 在覆盖 83 种语言的五项任务中，扩展效应主要表现为局部提升，即新增语言和文字系统获得的收益最明显。\n- 增大模型容量能够缓解语言数量增加导致的“多语言诅咒”，但对未见文字系统的迁移能力并未带来进一步扩展收益。\n- 对类型学差异较大的低资源语言，MPIXEL 在多种书写系统上优于子词级和字符级基线；分析还发现，更大的模型会保留更多层进行视觉处理，而跨语言表征对齐会随语言数量增加而减弱。"
purpose = "- 为无固定词表的多语言系统提供预训练扩展规律，可指导语言、文字系统与模型容量之间的资源配置。\n- 表明增加容量是缓解多语言性能竞争的有效手段，但不能简单解决未见文字系统迁移的上限问题。\n- 支持在类型学距离较远、资源稀缺或书写系统多样的场景中采用像素级文本建模。\n- 提示后续工作需重点改善语言规模扩展时的跨语言表征对齐。"
notes = "标题：Scaling Multilingual Pretraining of Pixel Language Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29681\n摘要：Pixel language models process text as rendered images instead of a finite vocabulary, which gives them strong cross-lingual transfer abilities. In this paper, we train a family of MPIXEL models along three axes of variation: more languages, more scripts, and larger model capacity under fixed per-language compute. In evaluations on five tasks covering 83 languages, we find that scaling acts locally: each axis improves the added languages and scripts, while increased capacity mitigates the curse of multilinguality from pretraining on more languages. Transfer to unseen scripts improves with multilingual pretraining, but does not further improve with scaling. On typologically distant low-resource l"

content = "论文关注同一条 LLM 生成记录中正确事实与幻觉、有效回答与刻板偏见并存时，传统数据清洗和单一评判模型难以处理的问题。作者提出 BiasGuard-D，让来自不同模型家族的两个对抗角色交换类型化主张文档，再由三个模型组成的仲裁组通过多数投票形成裁决。系统结合指称一致性与语义不变量进行确定性验证，并保存每项主张、投票和指令，以形成完整的证据溯源记录。该机制仅调整角色设定即可从刻板偏见修复迁移至 HaluEval QA 的幻觉修复，使正确答案恢复率提高 49 个百分点；消融迭代环节会显著降低不变量满足率。"
purpose = "- 为生产信息系统中的 LLM 生成记录提供可审计的质量控制层，区分真实修复与一概压制。\n- 通过跨模型家族审计降低单一评判模型共享训练偏差所造成的漏检风险。\n- 类型化主张、投票和指令记录可支持证据溯源、合规审计与故障归因。\n- 指称检查与语义不变量的组合适合用于检测局部正确但整体存在幻觉或偏见的复杂输出。"
notes = "标题：Conflicted Records: Typed Adversarial Contracts for Auditable LLM Output Quality Enforcement\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29669\n摘要：LLM-generated records increasingly populate production information systems where they are treated as data assets subject to the same quality expectations as records from operational databases. A single such record can simultaneously satisfy and violate a quality rule: a grounded fact adjacent to a hallucination, a useful answer adjacent to a stereotype. Classical data cleaning cannot resolve this because the two judgments are incompatible over the same span; a single LLM judge cannot either, since it shares the training biases that produced the conflict. Existing interventions such as prompt engineering, chain-of-thought, and RLHF reduce surface error rates but provide no audit trail and cannot distinguish genuine remediation from blanket suppression. The question is whether a deterministi"

content = "该工作研究利用LLM作为语义变异算子的公式化Alpha因子挖掘，指出传统LLM-EA容易出现搜索方向不足和因子结构同质化。LineageEvo将连续演化的因子变体组织为谱系，并从谱系范围内的搜索经验提炼算子先验，持续指导LLM生成。其谱系分层搜索分配机制在因子质量与谱系集中度之间进行权衡，避免少数分支主导演化。CSI 500与S&P 500上的实验显示，该方法提升了因子预测能力、投资组合表现和搜索效率，并获得更均衡的谱系分布与更丰富的因子结构。"
purpose = "- 将谱系作为个体与种群之间的中间控制单元，可改善LLM驱动进化搜索的方向性。\n- 谱系搜索经验能够转化为持续、局部相关的变异先验，减少随机探索带来的预算浪费。\n- 按谱系分配搜索预算有助于抑制优势分支垄断，兼顾候选质量与结构多样性。\n- 该框架可为量化金融中的符号因子发现及其他LLM辅助进化任务提供参考。"
notes = "标题：Lineage-Aware Search Control for LLM-Based Evolutionary Factor Mining\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29663\n摘要：Formulaic alpha factor mining searches for symbolic expressions that transform market data into predictive signals. Recent LLM-based evolutionary factor mining methods use large language models as semantic variation operators. Under the conventional LLM-EA paradigm, stochastic variation and fitness-based selection can leave the search weakly directed and concentrated around a narrow set of structurally similar factors. We argue that these limitations arise because current methods mainly organize search at the individual and population levels, leaving lineage-level control underused. A lineage connects successive factor variants through evolutionary descent. We propose LineageEvo, which uses lineage in two coupled forms of search control: (1) lineage-conditioned LLM variation, which distill"

id = "spare-moe"

summary = "SPARE 利用校准数据上的路由熵，在无需训练且不依赖专家选择器的情况下逐层分配专家剪枝比例，显著降低 MoE 内存占用并优于均匀剪枝。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29662"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "SPARE 以 Token 归一化 Top-K 门控权重的熵衡量各层路由分散程度，并据此决定每层应移除的专家数量。路由越分散，单个活跃专家被移除造成的信息损失通常越小，因此该层可采用更高的剪枝比例。该方法无需额外训练、与具体专家选择器无关，并通过随机剪枝扰动界和受控实验验证其理论直觉。在剪除 50% 专家时，Qwen3-30B-A3B 的八任务平均准确率由 57.2% 提升至 63.8%，DeepSeek-R1 的数学、代码和科学平均成绩由 80.1 提升至 82.8。"
purpose = "- 为 MoE 专家剪枝提供非均匀、按层自适应的稀疏预算分配策略。\n- 可在不重新训练模型的前提下降低内存占用和部署成本。\n- 路由熵可作为评估不同 MoE 层剪枝敏感度的低成本指标。\n- 对高剪枝率场景尤其有效，可用于 Mixtral、Qwen3 和 DeepSeek 等 MoE 模型的轻量化部署。"
notes = "标题：SPARE: Sparsity Allocation via Routing Entropy for Efficient MoEs\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29662\n摘要：Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale model capacity efficiently, yet their substantial memory footprint poses a major barrier to deployment. Expert pruning reduces this footprint, but most methods focus on which experts to remove and prune every layer equally. We propose SPARE (Sparsity Allocation via Routing Entropy), a training-free, selector-agnostic method that decides how many experts to remove from each layer using routing entropy, the entropy of tokens' normalized Top-K gate weights on calibration data. When a token's gate weight is spread evenly, removing one active expert discards little of it, so SPARE prunes layers with more diffuse routing more aggressively. A perturbation bound under random pruning formalizes this intuition, and controlled interventions with a fixed se"

summary = "研究以四足机器人导航为例检验“配对指令依赖”能否有效排序 VLA 示范语料，发现该指标虽可靠且具有预测性，但压缩为无符号切换率后未能显著提升语料选择决策。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29646"

content = "研究将机器人示范语料选择视为排序问题，要求上游诊断指标能够保留下游策略优劣次序，而不只是与平均性能相关。作者提出配对指令依赖指标，测量仅改变目标时示范者首个决策发生变化的比例，并在 136 个独立随机种子的四足机器人导航语料上进行前瞻性验证。该指标具有可靠性，且与下游配对正确性相关，加入基线后也降低了平均绝对预测误差；但主要对数评分增量的置信区间跨越零，预设的前 20% 语料比较亦未证明额外选择收益。探索性分析表明，将“均正确”和“均错误”响应分别建模，比使用无符号切换统计量获得更大的留出集收益，说明诊断指标需要保留与结果相关的反事实响应结构。"
purpose = "- 为 VLA 与具身智能训练中的机器人示范语料筛选提供面向下游决策价值的评估范式。\n- 提醒数据策展者区分“统计相关或可预测”与“能够改善候选排序和选择决策”两类证据。\n- 表明将反事实响应压缩为无符号切换率可能损失关键结构，应分别刻画正确与错误响应。\n- 可用于设计经过留出语料、随机种子一致性及实际选择端点验证的数据质量诊断指标。"
notes = "标题：From Association to Decision Value: Testing Paired Instruction Dependence for VLA Corpus Selection\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29646\n摘要：Choosing among robot demonstration corpora is a ranking problem: an upstream diagnostic must preserve which candidates produce better downstream policies, not merely correlate with average performance.
