notes = "标题：FogTTA: Online Test-Time Adaptation for Robust Transformer-based Object Detection in Foggy Weather\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29618\n摘要：Object detection models for autonomous driving commonly experience substantial performance drops when deployed under adverse weather due to the domain shift between training data and real-world operating conditions. Using RF-DETR as the underlying object detector, FogTTA enables real-time adaptation to the streaming target domain without requiring source data or retraining. The framework follows a teacher-student design, where the deployed model serves as the teacher and gene"

notes = "标题：No Frames, Just Frequencies: Bandwidth-Efficient Deployment-Time Adaptation for Smart Glasses\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29607\n摘要：Deep neural networks deployed on smart glasses must adapt to changes in the distribution of incoming data while adhering to strict privacy and device constraints. Rather than transmitting camera frames, the glasses compute and send frequency descriptors to a host device that contains a replica of the model. The host device maps the received frequency descriptor to a distribution shift operator that synthesizes out-of-distribution data and adapts a model replica using a pre-defined adaptation protocol. We evaluate the framework on mixtures of ImageNet-C corruptions using convolution-based (ConvNeXtV2"

summary = "DRiC通过递归更新DCT域岭回归的充分统计量，在标签延迟条件下以较小状态开销持续校正冻结多变量预测器的残差。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29604"

content = "DRiC将预测值、最近输入块及下一残差块压缩为前若干个离散余弦变换（DCT）系数，并通过递归岭回归实现在线校正。局部版本DRiC-L为每个通道维护独立回归器，全球版本DRiC-G跨通道共享回归器，使其统计量规模不随通道数增长。安全守卫根据各通道残差的运行尺度跳过异常数据块更新，并裁剪输出校正量，以增强缺失值或异常编码下的稳定性。在7个数据集、3类预测骨干和126种条件上的实验中，DRiC-L8平均降低14.55%的MSE，并以显著更少的状态超过最强对比方法ELF的平均改进幅度；DRiC-G则在Electricity和Traffic上展示了较高的状态效率。"

However, under label delay the target of a block arrives only after the whole horizon has passed, and the corrector's retained state grows with the number of channels. Aiming to combine a small retained state with a large error reduction, we propose DRiC (DCT Ridge Corrector), which fits a ridge regression of the leading discrete cosine transform (DCT) coefficients of the next residual block on those of the frozen forecast and of the latest input block, with sufficient statistics updated recursively. Its local variant, DRiC-L, keeps one such regression per channel; its global variant, DRiC-G, pools all channels into one regression, whose statistics do not depend on the channel count. A guard tracks a running "

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29602"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究基于 ToTTo 数据集，对掩码扩散语言模型 MDLM、LLaDA 与自回归模型 GPT-2、GPT-Neo 进行统一的检索式少样本评测，并单独衡量参考相似度与源表格事实支撑。LLaDA 的示例数量增加后，BLEU 从 11.3 升至 22.0，但相对于源表格的 NLI 蕴含分数从 0.78 降至 0.52。经 QLoRA 适配的 LLaDA 获得最高 BLEU 24.15，却未表现出更好的事实落地能力。人工标注显示，自回归模型更容易生成表格之外的内容，而 LLaDA 系模型通常保持主题相关，但更常替换具体实体。"
purpose = "- 表明 BLEU 等参考文本相似度指标不足以单独评估表格到文本生成的事实忠实性。\n- 建议同时采用源表格蕴含评测与人工错误分析，区分整体离题和实体替换等失效模式。\n- 提醒少样本示例增加或 QLoRA 微调可能改善文本匹配分数，却不一定增强结构化输入的事实 grounding。\n- 为扩散语言模型与自回归语言模型的可靠性比较提供统一诊断框架。"
notes = "标题：A Diagnostic Study of Faithfulness in Diffusion Language Models for Table-to-Text Generation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29602\n摘要：Diffusion language models generate by iteratively denoising a masked sequence under bidirectional attention, without the strict left-to-right commitment of autoregressive decoding. Whether this difference affects how reliably they remain grounded in structured inputs is largely unexplored. We study this question in table-to-text generation using ToTTo, where highlighted source cells make factual grounding directly testable. We compare the masked diffusion models MDLM and LLaDA with GPT-2 and GPT-Neo under an identical retrieval-based few-shot protocol, together with a QLoRA-adapted LLaDA, and we score reference similarity and source grounding as separate axes. The two pull apart. Within LLaDA, additional demonstrations raise BLEU from 11.3 to 22.0 while NLI entailment against the source ta"

summary = "论文以反身性矩阵刻画多智能体间的交叉反馈，证明其谱半径跨越 1 时，重复重训练会由几何收敛转变为最坏情形下的线性损失增长。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29577"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文研究多智能体实施性预测中的重复重训练，其中各智能体部署后的预测会经交互网络改变其他智能体未来面对的数据分布。作者提出反身性矩阵 Γ，利用逐智能体的梯度敏感性和曲率参数描述一个智能体对另一个智能体最优响应的影响强度。当 Γ 的谱半径小于 1 时，重训练过程几何收敛至唯一的实施性稳定均衡，且累积超额损失不随时间跨度增长。当谱半径超过 1 时，作者构造了带噪声的超临界实例，证明仅使用局部信息的学习器会在特定时间范围内遭受线性增长的期望累积超额损失。"
purpose = "- 为分析网络化学习系统中的部署反馈与训练稳定性提供矩阵化工具，避免将复杂的跨智能体作用压缩为单一收缩常数。\n- 可通过估计反身性矩阵的谱半径，判断多智能体重复重训练是否处于稳定区域。\n- 启示系统设计者通过削弱关键交互边、调整网络拓扑或增加正则化，将谱半径控制在 1 以下。\n- 为金融、推荐和社会决策等存在策略性反馈的多主体在线学习系统提供风险预警依据。"
notes = "标题：The Reflexivity Threshold: A Phase Transition for Multi-Agent Performative Prediction\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29577\n摘要：Many learning systems are performative: once deployed, their predictions change the data distribution on which future models are trained. In multi-agent settings, this feedback is routed through a network of interacting learners, so stability depends not only on the strength of performativity but also on who affects whom. We study repeated retraining in multi-agent performative prediction and identify a spectral threshold that separates stable learning from worst-case learning obstruction. Prior work often collapses cross-agent feedback into a scalar contraction constant, missing this network structure. We instead introduce the reflexivity matrix $Γ$, which records how strongly one agent's deployment can move another agent's best response, defined from per-agent gradient sensitivities and "

id = "dpo-1-4"

subtopic = "偏好优化"

score = 2

summary = "该研究通过分别随机剪枝 DPO 的评分位置、执行序列前缀和反向传播偏好分支，在降低峰值显存的同时实现最高 1.426 倍单步训练加速。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29538"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究提出统一的 DPO 计算图随机剪枝框架，在实际操作前分别采样用于评分的响应位置、用于执行的响应前缀以及参与反向传播的偏好分支。位置与序列剪枝主要降低峰值显存，而分支剪枝可直接省略部分反向传播计算。框架使用逆概率加权，为采样目标构造条件无偏的单分支梯度估计器。实验中，分支剪枝相较拼接式 DPO 基线获得 1.426 倍单步加速，并在两个模型家族上较标准 DPO 缩短约 30% 的训练时间，但平均准确率略有下降，开放式评测结果也因基准而异。"
purpose = "- 为 DPO 后训练提供可分阶段控制的计算与显存优化方案。\n- 表明仅减少监督信号并不必然节省计算，剪枝决策必须发生在评分、执行或反向传播操作之前。\n- 可利用逆概率加权在减少偏好分支计算时控制梯度估计偏差。\n- 实际部署时需权衡训练加速、峰值显存与下游准确率变化。"
notes = "标题：Stochastic Pruning of the DPO Computation Graph: 1.4x Faster Training and Lower Peak Memory\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29538\n摘要：Direct preference optimization (DPO) incurs training costs in output scoring, sequence execution, and backpropagation, so reducing supervision alone does not necessarily save computation. We introduce a unified framework for stochastic pruning of the DPO computation graph that independently samples response positions for scoring, response prefixes for execution, and preference branches for backpropagation. By making these selections before the corresponding operations, the framework omits computation at each stage of a training step. Our analysis characterizes the objective changes induced by position and sequence pruning and uses inverse-probability weighting to obtain a conditionally unbiased single-branch gradient estimator for the sampled objective. Controlled measurements show that po"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文以知识空间和注意力配对的几何形式统一推导多类注意力机制，并探索可启发新型神经网络架构的几何空间。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29526"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出一种从几何视角推导注意力机制与神经网络架构的统一框架。作者首先形式化定义“知识世界”和“注意力配对”，将信息交互过程表示为几何结构。该框架可自然导出标准注意力、因果注意力、线性注意力、张量注意力以及循环神经网络。论文还构造了若干新的几何空间，为设计潜在的新型 Transformer 与神经网络架构提供思路。"
purpose = "- 为不同注意力变体提供统一的几何解释，有助于比较其结构假设与信息交互方式。\n- 可利用新的几何空间系统化搜索注意力算子及神经网络架构，而非仅依赖经验设计。\n- 为研究线性注意力、张量注意力与循环结构之间的联系提供形式化工具。"
notes = "标题：Geometric knowledge spaces, Attention pairing and Architectures\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29526\n摘要：In this paper, we propose a geometric approach to deriving attention mechanisms and exploring potential new transformer architectures. We begin by formalizing the notions of knowledge worlds and attention pairings, then demonstrate how this framework naturally leads to the architectures of standard attention, causal attention, linear attention, tensor attention, and recurrent neural networks. Finally, we introduce several new geometric spaces that may inspire novel neural network architectures."


[[items]]
id = "pplx-embed-v2-late"

score = 2

summary = "Perplexity AI 发布 0.6B 与 9B 两款多语言多模态后期交互模型，通过从 18B 教师进行表征蒸馏，实现同尺寸领先的检索性能与非对称部署。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29523"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "pplx-embed-v2-late 包含 0.6B 和 9B 两种规模，面向文本、图像及视觉文档检索，并支持多语言输入。两款模型在各自参数规模的多项检索基准上取得领先结果，其中 0.6B 版本在视觉文档检索中可与大数倍的模型竞争。模型通过一个 18B 教师执行表征蒸馏，使不同规模版本共享统一嵌入空间。该设计支持非对称部署：使用 9B 模型离线索引文档，同时使用低延迟的 0.6B 模型处理在线查询。"
purpose = "- 为多模态检索系统提供兼顾召回质量、在线延迟与部署成本的模型组合。\n- 共享嵌入空间允许索引端和查询端采用不同规模模型，无需重复构建文档索引。\n- 展示表征蒸馏在跨规模检索模型能力迁移中的价值，可用于构建低延迟搜索与视觉文档检索服务。"
notes = "标题：pplx-embed-v2-late Technical Report\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29523\n摘要：In this report, we introduce pplx-embed-v2-late, a pair of 0.6B and 9B multilingual and multimodal late-interaction models. Both models achieve state-of-the-art results within their respective size classes across a range of text, image, and visual document retrieval benchmarks. On visual document retrieval tasks, pplx-embed-v2-late-0.6b is competitive with models several times larger than itself. The models are trained via representation distillation from an 18B teacher. Consequently, they share an embedding space that supports asymmetric deployment: documents can be indexed with the 9B model and queried with the low-latency 0.6B model. Both models are available at https://huggingface.co/collections/perplexity-ai/pplx-embed-v2."

summary = "论文提出免训练的理由对比解码（RCD），通过增强由推理理由支持的词元，提高答案对思维链内容的真实依赖并缓解查询捷径。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29518"
code = ""

content = "论文研究思维链生成中最终答案绕过推理理由、直接依赖查询特征的捷径行为。作者将上下文感知解码应用于思维链，提出理由对比解码（RCD），对比有无理由条件下的下一词元分布，增强因理由而提高概率的词元。该解码规则可解释为一个带 KL 正则化的奖励最大化目标。在五个推理基准上的实验表明，RCD 相比标准 CoT 提高了答案准确率；基于真实与随机理由的干预分析还显示，最终答案对理由内容的行为依赖得到增强。"
purpose = "- 为缓解思维链中的捷径学习提供简单、免训练的测试时解码方案。\n- 可通过对比有无推理理由的词元分布，提高答案与中间推理过程的一致性。\n- 提示思维链系统不应只评估最终准确率，还应利用理由干预检验答案是否真正依赖推理内容。\n- KL 正则化奖励最大化视角可为后续设计可控、忠实的对比解码策略提供理论依据。"
notes = "标题：Mitigating Query Shortcuts in Chain-of-Thought with Context-Aware Decoding\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29518\n摘要：While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting improves the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs), recent studies have shown that final answers do not always depend on the generated rationales. In particular, LLMs may rely on query-specific shortcuts rather than the information provided by their reasoning steps. In this paper, we investigate whether context-aware decoding (CAD), an existing contrastive decoding method, can mitigate such shortcut behavior when applied to CoT reasoning. We study this application as Rationale Contrastive Decoding (RCD), which contrasts the next-token distributions conditioned on the query with and without its rationale, amplifying tokens whose likelihood increases due to the rationale. We provide a theoretical interpretation of this decoding rule via "


[[items]]
id = "bridge"
title = "BRIDGE：阻断模型更新虚假改进的接受门控"
subtopic = "模型可靠性"
topics = [
    "模型评估",
    "人工智能可靠性",

    "不确定性估计",

    "医疗人工智能评测",
]
score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "BRIDGE通过交叉拟合与 bootstrap 增益下界构建模型无关的更新接受门控，在多类数据及回顾性临床任务中将虚假改进率显著降至接近零。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29515"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "BRIDGE针对模型反复更新中“验证集表现提升、但新数据表现未改善”的虚假改进问题，为每次更新设置统一的接受门控。该方法采用交叉拟合，让各数据折评判由其他折选出的更新，从而降低使用同一数据选择和评价更新所产生的选择偏差。只有当性能增益的 bootstrap 置信下界超过预设边际时，更新才会被接受。在新数据集、合成任务、12项回顾性临床任务及OpenML数据集上，BRIDGE将虚假改进率由约12.5%–25.6%降至0%–1.7%，并移除了朴素规则所接受虚假改进的97%–100%。"
purpose = "- 可作为模型持续迭代或部署流水线中的更新准入机制，避免仅凭验证集正向增益上线新版本。\n- 交叉拟合将更新选择与更新评判分离，可减少选择偏差和验证集过拟合。\n- 基于 bootstrap 下界和安全边际作出保守决策，适合临床等高风险应用的版本验收。\n- 该结果提示模型更新评估不仅应比较平均性能，还应显式控制虚假改进率及其置信上界。"
notes = "标题：BRIDGE: An Acceptance Gate to Block False Improvements in Model Updates, with a Retrospective Clinical Evaluation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29515\n摘要：Models are updated repeatedly, and an update that looks better on validation data but not on new data is a false improvement that can misguide clinical decisions. The naive rule accepts every positive validation gain and is optimistic when the same data choose and judge the update. BRIDGE is a model-agnostic acceptance gate that decides each update from predictions and the available labels, with fixed settings. Each fold of the data judges the update chosen on the other folds (cross-fitting) to reduce selection bias; acceptance requires the gain's lower bootstrap bound to exceed a margin. The four settings are (A) 11 new datasets, (B) synthetic tasks, (C) 12 retrospective clinical tasks, and (D) 39 supporting datasets. The false-improvement rate falls from 19.1% (naive) to 1.1% (BRIDGE) in"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29507"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究检验了将时序推理划分为“时间算术”与“时间语义”两类能力的常见假设。针对相应子集进行微调，可在原数据集上带来 6.8 至 23.1 个百分点的提升，但同类任务跨数据集迁移仅为 -3.2 至 1.1 个百分点，显示收益主要具有数据集特异性。基于认知需求的评估进一步表明，定量推理和逻辑推理并非基准表现的主要驱动因素，知识因素反而更关键。结果说明现有基准更多奖励模型记忆时间运作的一般规律，而未能可靠测量可迁移的时序推理能力。"
purpose = "- 提醒基准设计者不要默认时间算术与时间语义对应两种独立且可迁移的能力。\n- 评估时应加入跨数据集迁移实验，以区分真实能力提升与数据集特异性适配。\n- 可依据认知需求重新标注和构建任务，减少背景知识记忆对时序推理测量的干扰。\n- 为分析时序推理基准的构念效度、测量效度与跨域泛化提供实证依据。"
notes = "标题：Keeping Time vs. Understanding Time: Neither Alone Explains Temporal Reasoning in LLMs\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29507\n摘要：Temporal reasoning is widely assumed to decompose into two modes: temporal arithmetic (date and time calculations), and temporal semantics (understanding temporal and causal relations). This dichotomy shapes how benchmarks are designed, how models are trained, and how progress is measured, yet its empirical validity has never been directly tested. We provide two lines of evidence that challenge it. First, we test whether tasks labelled as arithmetic or semantic by the benchmarks support transferable gains within their purported mode. Finetuning on these subsets yields predominantly dataset-specific gains ($+6.8$ to $+23.1$ percentage points), while transfer to a different dataset in the same mode ranges from $-3.2$ to $+1.1$ percentage points. Across the two benchmark families used in the "

summary = "FairAMO 通过冻结候选预测结果，发现智能体模型编排对时间序列预测的提升不足 6%，且收益主要取决于候选模型的互补性而非数量。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29504"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出受控评估协议 FairAMO，通过冻结候选时间序列基础模型的预测结果，区分候选模型质量与编排策略本身的贡献。在 GIFT-Eval 上，智能体模型编排能够提高预测准确率，但最佳编排器相较最佳单一 TSFM 的 MASE 降幅在所有任务上均低于 6%。收益主要来自候选模型在不同任务上的互补性，而非候选数量；继续增加成员会产生边际收益递减，冗余成员甚至可能抵消已有提升。历史证据的价值也会快速饱和，仅使用少量近期窗口即可获得全部收益。"
purpose = "- 为评估模型编排系统提供控制候选质量与预测结果的公平实验协议。\n- 提醒系统设计者优先提升候选模型的互补性，而非盲目扩大候选池。\n- 有助于准确归因时间序列预测提升，避免将候选模型优势误判为编排能力。\n- 表明近期短窗口已足以支持编排决策，可减少历史上下文与推理成本。"
notes = "标题：Rethinking Agentic Model Orchestration for Time Series Forecasting\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29504\n摘要：Time series foundation models (TSFMs) perform unevenly across forecasting tasks. Recent work uses agentic model orchestration (AMO) to select or combine TSFMs, often outperforming individual forecasters. Yet these gains may reflect stronger or more complementary candidates rather than better orchestration. To disentangle these effects, we introduce FairAMO, a controlled evaluation protocol with frozen candidate forecasts. Experiments on GIFT-Eval show that AMO improves accuracy, but its gains are bounded by the candidate TSFMs. The best orchestrators reduce MASE by less than 6% relative to the best single TSFM across all tasks. These gains depend on complementarity (different candidates excel on different tasks) rather than the number of candidates. Adding members yields diminishing return"

notes = "标题：OmniSimulator: Aligning Small Language Models for Authentic Heterogeneous Behavior Modeling\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29472\n摘要：Large language models (LLMs) have shown great potential as general-purpose user simulators for interactive systems. However, the upper bound of user simulation is still constrained by the model's reasoning paradigm: native chain-of-thought often degenerates into shallow pattern matching and fails to adequately model the behavioral factors underlying real user decisions. In this paper, we propose OmniSimulator, a novel training framework designed to improve the predictive ability of small LLMs for realistic user simulation on short-video platform environments. Specifically, OmniSimulator organizes behavior-oriented reasoning through a structured schem"

content = "RegoBench 是首个自动化 Rego 策略修复基准，收录了 1,159 个真实世界缺陷。RegoMender 采用反馈引导的多智能体架构，各智能体通过共享黑板和原子工具协作，动态执行补丁生成、重写与回滚。该框架在 RegoBench 上取得 82.9% 的语法修复成功率和 77.9% 的测试语义成功率，较最强基线提升 13.4%。它在单步及链式修复任务中均保持有效，并可泛化至 HCL 和 SQL，在 132 个真实 CVE 漏洞上的修复率达到 86.4%。"
purpose = "- 为策略即代码（PaC）场景提供面向 Rego 特性的自动程序修复方案，减少人工试错。\n- 展示共享黑板、细粒度工具调用与动态回滚对复杂链式修复任务的价值。\n- RegoBench 可用于评估代码智能体在声明式策略语言上的语法修复与语义修复能力。\n- 跨 HCL、SQL 和真实 CVE 的结果表明，该框架可扩展至云原生配置及安全漏洞治理。"
notes = "标题：RegoMender: A Multi-agent Framework for Automated Rego Policy Repair\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29456\n摘要：Policy-as-Code (PaC) has emerged as a foundational paradigm in cloud-native systems. However, repairing Rego policies remains challenging due to their strict syntax, declarative semantics, and specific verification requirements. Existing automated program repair (APR) methods are mainly designed for mainstream languages and struggle to accommodate the unique policy logic and repair workflows of Rego. Thus, Rego policy repair remains a manual process driven by trial and error. To address this, we introduce RegoBench, the first automated Rego policy benchmark comprising 1,159 real-world defects, and propose RegoMender, a feedback-guided multi-agent framework. Collaborating via a shared blackboard and an atomic toolkit, RegoMender dynamically executes patching, rewriting, or rolling back, to "

id = "item-147"
title = "事件而非像素：世界模型的回报误差诊断"
subtopic = "世界模型"

score = 2

summary = "研究提出转移级诊断方法，发现世界模型的规划回报误差主要集中在奖励或终止事件，而传统像素指标难以识别这些关键失真。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29435"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "作者通过用真实转移替换想象转移，并与等预算随机替换对照，衡量各步骤对回报误差的贡献。奖励产生或回合终止的事件转移在像素层面并不比其他步骤差，但修复这些转移可消除中位数约 70% 的回报误差。相反，按 MSE、SSIM 或 LPIPS 选出的最大视觉误差转移，其修复效果甚至低于随机选择。该结论在通过可靠性检查的 44 个 Atari-100k 模型—数据集合组合中具有显著一致性，并可用于改善计划排序及预测扩散世界模型的修复效果。"
purpose = "- 提醒世界模型评估不应仅依赖帧重建质量，应直接测量关键事件对回报预测与计划排序的影响。\n- 可将转移级真实替换诊断用于定位奖励事件、终止事件及其时间偏移造成的规划失效。\n- 为世界模型修复和训练数据分配提供依据，优先提升稀疏奖励事件附近的动力学与回报建模能力。\n- 可据此设计面向控制性能的事件敏感指标，补充 MSE、SSIM 和 LPIPS 等视觉指标。"
notes = "标题：Events, Not Pixels: Where World Models Get the Return Wrong\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29435\n摘要：World models are increasingly used for planning, where a planner ranks candidate actions by returns imagined by the model. However, these models are judged mainly by how well they render frames, even though a visually faithful rollout can still assign the wrong return. To identify where this mismatch arises, we introduce a transition-level diagnostic that replaces imagined transitions with real ones and measures the reduction in return error against a budget-matched random control. Our analysis yields two findings. (1) Event transitions, the few steps where a reward is earned or an episode ends, are rendered as well as other steps (median pixel-error ratio 0.85), yet restoring them removes a median of 70% of the return error. In contrast, restoring transitions with the largest MSE, SSIM or"

id = "item-148"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文以样本对留出查询节省的编码长度为信息价值，提出可认证且误差受控的规则，用于判断新数据应置于模型上下文还是写入权重。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29419"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文建立了一条适用于任意架构、数据集和任务的信息价值精确关系，将留出查询上节省的编码长度分解为查询相关存储比特、先验修复项与解码损失。基于该关系的上界，方法可从单次测量中认证数据更适合进入上下文还是通过微调写入权重，并通过 anytime-valid 检验控制停止时的错误率。实验覆盖 Transformer、TabPFN、占用网络和立体视觉网络，并用于比较 GPT-2 缓存与微调、TabPFN 与 XGBoost，以及上下文学习器与训练后代理模型。该无标签估值还可诊断上下文中哪些证据真正有效，绘制上下文学习曲线，并用于支持样本排序和证据裁决。"
purpose = "- 为上下文学习、缓存和参数微调之间的选择提供可量化、可认证的决策依据。\n- 可用留出查询在线评估新增上下文的信息价值，并在任意停止时间保持统计误差控制。\n- 有助于筛选高价值示例、压缩无效上下文，降低长上下文推理与重复微调的成本。\n- 将编码长度视角扩展到视觉、表格和科学代理任务，为跨架构的数据价值评估提供统一工具。"
notes = "标题：When Thoughts Become Memory: Unifying In-Context Learning and Fine-Tuning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29419\n摘要：We are the first to give a certified rule for whether unseen data belong in a pretrained model's context or its weights, built on an exact law for their information value. That value, the code length saved on held-out queries, equals in expectation the bits stored about the query, plus a prior repair, minus a decoding loss, for any model, architecture, dataset and task. The law's ceilings certify the choice from one measurement, and an anytime-valid test makes it from held-out queries, with error controlled whenever it stops. Every certificate the ceilings issue on a trained transformer, TabPFN, an occupancy network and a stereo network is correct. The test picks TabPFN over XGBoost and a trained surrogate over an in-context learner on airfoil pressure, calls the better of GPT-2's cache an"

title = "掼蛋自博弈中的预测—优化鸿沟"

topics = [

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究发现，掼蛋自博弈中能够有效预测比赛胜负进展的信号，并不必然构成稳定有益的强化学习优化方向。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29411"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究以多轮、不完全信息游戏掼蛋为环境，区分结果信号的预测有效性与其作为优化目标的实际效用。留出集分析表明，公共比赛状态与轮次得失之间的交互可提供额外预测价值，说明比赛进程具有预测意义。作者将冻结的比赛胜率预测器变化量 ΔΦ 作为轮级监督信号，嵌入匹配的 DanZero 风格蒙特卡洛自博弈流程；个别训练谱系中的增强检查点显著优于基线。然而，五个预注册复现实验种子均未取得正向结果，十种子汇总区间包含零，且跨迭代效果反复变号，揭示了预测信号与可靠优化收益之间的鸿沟。"
purpose = "- 提醒强化学习研究者不要将预测相关性直接等同于可优化性或因果改进方向。\n- 评估辅助奖励或进度信号时，应采用多随机种子、预注册复现和跨迭代稳定性分析。\n- 个别检查点或单条训练谱系的成功可能产生选择偏差，方法结论应以汇总实验为依据。\n- 对自博弈奖励设计而言，需要进一步验证信号是否与学习器动态、信用分配及策略更新方向相匹配。"
notes = "标题：The Predictive--Optimization Gap in GuanDan Self-Play\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29411\n摘要：Outcome-predictive signals are often treated as promising optimization targets in reinforcement learning, but predictive validity and optimization utility are different claims. We expose this distinction in GuanDan, a multi-round imperfect-information game in which the implication of a round outcome depends on the public match state. Held-out analysis shows that state--gain interactions provide incremental predictive value beyond a constant signed-gain effect, establishing that match progress is predictively meaningful. We then use changes in a frozen match-win predictor, $ΔΦ$, as a controlled round-level supervision intervention in a matched DanZero-style Monte Carlo self-play protocol. The intervention can succeed in an individual lineage: a selected progress-augmented checkpoint substan"


[[items]]
id = "echo"
title = "ECHO：偏好优化中的公共模式偏移导致词表坍缩"
subtopic = "偏好优化"
topics = [
    "偏好优化",
    "模型训练",
    "人工智能可靠性",

    "偏好优化",

]
score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "ECHO 揭示偏好优化会累积排序损失难以感知的公共模式隐藏状态偏移，并通过形成“词表势阱”最终引发单一词元主导的生成坍缩。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29410"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究发现，偏好优化可能在排序指标持续改善的同时，使不同位置、提示以及正负响应的隐藏状态共同沿同一方向偏移。由于该公共模式偏移同步作用于 chosen 与 rejected 表征，相对排序损失对其累积并不敏感。偏移投影到输出层后会形成“词表势阱”，不断抬高某个词元的概率，直至其主导解码并造成生成坍缩。实验在多种基础模型谱系、全参数微调以及 DPO、SimPO 设置中观察到这一现象，并表明屏蔽损失中的公共分量可以抑制坍缩。该表征级信号还能在实际坍缩前出现，可作为偏好优化训练的预警指标。"
purpose = "- 揭示仅依赖排序准确率或奖励模型偏好的评估可能掩盖严重的生成质量退化。\n- 可在训练期间监测隐藏状态的公共模式偏移及其与词表方向的对齐程度，提前预警生成坍缩。\n- 为 DPO、SimPO 等偏好优化算法设计公共分量屏蔽、表征正则化或早停机制提供依据。\n- 提醒实践者同时评估生成样本质量、表征漂移和词元概率集中度，而非只关注相对排名指标。"
notes = "标题：ECHO: Emergent Common-Mode Hidden Offsets Drive Vocabulary Collapse in Preference Optimization\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29410\n摘要：Preference optimization can improve every ranking metric while building a hidden, shared representation shift that the relative loss barely sees, one that can eventually destroy generation. We trace this failure to the common-mode offset, a shared directional shift in the model's hidden states that appears across token positions, prompts, and both chosen and rejected responses. Because it moves chosen and rejected representations together, the relative ranking loss barely reacts to its accumulation. As this offset grows, it projects onto the output layer to form a vocabulary well, elevating a single token's probability until it dominates decoding and causes generation collapse. The isolated offset alone reconstructs the vocabulary well, injecting the learned offset into an untreated base m"

summary = "PLRM通过可并行的变分框架与混合先验建模多种潜在推理轨迹，在提升训练效率的同时避免不同推理策略坍缩为单一分布。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29385"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "PLRM将显式、逐词生成的思维链迁移到紧凑连续的潜空间中，并以概率分布表示多条有效推理轨迹。该框架采用JEPA式目标，后验网络从已观测推理轨迹并行推断潜变量分布，先验网络则通过教师强制预测后续潜分布。为表达异质推理过程，作者引入离散策略变量和混合先验，保留多条轨迹形成的高斯混合结构，降低分布坍缩风险。实验显示，PLRM在单轨迹数据集上可媲美确定性潜推理模型，并在多轨迹数据集上持续优于其他随机基线。"
purpose = "- 为推理模型提供一种不依赖冗长语言化思维链的紧凑轨迹表示方式。\n- 通过并行后验推断缓解自回归潜变量训练耗时过长的问题。\n- 混合先验和离散策略变量可用于生成、保留并探索具有实质差异的多样化推理路径。\n- 为潜空间推理中的策略搜索、候选生成及测试时并行计算提供基础。"
notes = "标题：Parallelizable Probabilistic Latent Reasoning for Diverse Reasoning Trajectories\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29385\n摘要：Language models relying on verbalized Chain-of-Thought (CoT) are constrained to token-by-token generation in the language space, where many tokens serve textual coherence rather than core reasoning. This entangles genuine differences in logical trajectories with superficial variations in verbalization. Moving reasoning into a compact, continuous latent space offers a promising alternative for efficiently representing and exploring diverse reasoning trajectories. However, current latent models remain largely deterministic, capturing only a single reasoning trajectory rather than modeling a distribution over multiple valid alternatives. While stochastic approaches parameterize distributions over latent reasoning steps, they frequently suffer from unstable optimization and limited empirical p"

summary = "OnQ 通过在策略蒸馏与候选级 Dynamic KL 缓解量化学生模型的轨迹偏移和概率分布失配，显著恢复低比特模型的长程推理能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29378"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "OnQ 面向 2～3 比特量化模型在长程推理中易偏离教师轨迹、超出词元预算的问题，让学生模型生成自己的响应，并由全精度教师在学生实际访问的前缀上提供监督。候选级 Dynamic KL 根据教师与学生对每个候选词元的概率比，动态组合正向与反向 KL，以同时纠正概率低估和高估。该流程从分块校准开始，再进行基于自生成轨迹的量化感知训练。在 Qwen3-1.7B、4B 和 8B 的九项基准上，OnQ 获得最高平均成绩；其中 2 比特 Qwen3-8B 相比最强基线提升 10.26 分，并生成更短、截断与重复错误更少的回答。"
purpose = "- 为低比特推理模型提供一种降低训练轨迹与部署轨迹分布偏移的量化感知训练方案。\n- 表明在学生自身前缀上实施教师监督，比固定响应序列蒸馏更适合长程推理能力恢复。\n- Dynamic KL 可用于精细纠正不同候选词元的概率高估与低估，改善量化后的分布对齐。\n- 在压缩模型计算与存储成本的同时，可减少冗长输出、预算内未终止和重复生成问题。"
notes = "标题：Right On Q: Recovering Reasoning under Quantization via Self-Generated Trajectories\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29378\n摘要：Quantization-aware training (QAT) aims to recover reasoning capabilities in low-bit large language models (LLMs). Existing recipes typically supervise the quantized student with the original full-precision model on fixed response sequences. However, the resulting low-bit quantized models still struggle on long-horizon reasoning tasks and often fail to terminate within a token budget that suffices for the full-precision model. We identify two challenges in closing this gap. First, fixed-response supervision leaves a mismatch between training prefixes and those encountered during student generation, and this trajectory divergence grows as responses lengthen. Second, on its own prefixes, the student underpredicts some next-token candidates and overpredicts others, and this distributional misa"

id = "gap-moe"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "GaP-MoE通过累积并正交投影专家输出梯度，在不修改任务损失的情况下减少专家功能重叠并缓解稀疏MoE的表示坍缩。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29355"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "GaP-MoE针对稀疏混合专家模型中多个专家趋向相似功能的表示坍缩问题，直接干预专家输出梯度，而非改造路由器或添加辅助损失。每个训练步骤依据归一化路由分数，从Top-K专家中随机选择一个领导专家，并累积其近期梯度。其余跟随专家的梯度被投影至领导专家梯度子空间的正交补，从而消除专家间的梯度方向重叠，并保持原始任务目标不变。该方法在基于BERT的GLUE微调以及基于GPT-2的WikiText-103、enwik8和text8预训练实验中，取得了与标准SMoE相当或更优的性能，并可作为现有SMoE架构的即插即用模块。"
purpose = "- 为缓解MoE专家同质化提供一种无需辅助损失的优化路径，避免特化约束与原始任务目标发生冲突。\n- 可将正交梯度投影用于提升专家多样性，并研究其对路由稳定性、负载均衡和规模扩展的影响。\n- 作为即插即用训练模块，适合在现有稀疏MoE架构及预训练、微调流程中进行低侵入式验证。"
notes = "标题：GaP-MoE : Explicit Expert Specialization in Mixture-of-Experts via Orthogonal Gradient Accumulation and Projection\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29355\n摘要：Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) models improve scalability by routing input tokens to only a subset of expert networks, but they often suffer from representation collapse, in which multiple experts converge toward similar functions and lose their intended diversity. Prior work has addressed this limitation either by improving router architectures or by introducing auxiliary loss terms that implicitly penalize similarity among experts. However, router-based approaches remain vulnerable to routing collapses, and loss-based approaches can conflict with the task's original objective during optimization. In this work, we propose GaP-MoE (Gradient Accumulation and Projection in MoE), which induces expert specialization by directly manipulating expert output gradients rather than modifying the l"

score = 2

summary = "研究建立长上下文持续预训练的经验缩放定律，表明其性能收益随训练规模可预测增长，且在约1000亿训练词元范围内尚未出现明显饱和。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29352"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "研究提出加性幂律，将长上下文性能与模型规模、短上下文预训练词元预算及长上下文持续预训练（LCCP）词元预算联系起来。该规律在五种模型规模和一百余种训练预算配置上得到验证，并能外推至未参与拟合的模型规模。作者还提出 Constant-Last4 协议，通过平均恒定学习率训练中最近四个连续检查点，稳定监测单条可扩展训练轨迹。对 1.22B 参数模型约 100B LCCP 词元的实验显示性能持续提升且未明显饱和，收益在扩展至 128K 上下文和监督微调后仍可迁移至长上下文理解与智能体任务。"
purpose = "- 为长上下文持续预训练的词元预算分配和停止时机提供可量化依据。\n- 可利用经验幂律预测不同模型规模与训练预算下的长上下文性能，降低大规模试验成本。\n- Constant-Last4 可用于平滑检查点波动，更稳定地观察持续预训练收益。\n- 结果提示不应因短期波动过早终止 LCCP，其收益能够保留到上下文扩展、监督微调及下游智能体任务中。"
notes = "标题：Don't Stop Long-Context Continual Pre-Training Too Early: An Empirical Scaling Law\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29352\n摘要：Long-context continual pre-training (LCCP) is widely used to improve long-context capability, but how its benefits scale with the number of tokens used for LCCP remains unclear. We investigate this question through modeling, monitoring, and validating the benefits of scaling LCCP. First, we establish an empirical additive power law relating long-context performance to model size and the respective token budgets of short-context pre-training and LCCP. Experiments across five model scales and more than one hundred short- and long-context budget configurations show that the fitted law reproduces the observed trends and extrapolates well to held-out model scales. Second, we introduce \\textsc{Constant-Last4}, a training and observation protocol that averages the four most recent consecutive che"

title = "数据调度与学习率调度应协同设计"
subtopic = "中期训练"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究表明，中期训练的数据混合策略与学习率调度存在强耦合，学习率是否衰减会改变最优的预训练与中期训练数据配比方案。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29348"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- 研究将从预训练数据完全切换至中期训练数据的常规做法，推广为训练过程中动态调整两类数据比例的数据混合调度问题。\n- 在总计算量和中期训练词元预算固定时，不同数据调度的优劣取决于学习率调度，单独改变学习率策略甚至会逆转比较结果。\n- 当学习率持续衰减时，提前引入高价值中期训练数据并与预训练数据混合，可比在训练末期集中使用取得更低的最终损失；采用恒定学习率时结论相反。\n- 消融实验显示，更合适的联合策略是在中期训练数据进入后再开始衰减学习率，理论模型进一步解释了两种调度之间的耦合机制。"
purpose = "- 指导中期训练流水线联合搜索数据引入时机、混合比例与学习率衰减起点，而非分别调优。\n- 在固定计算和高价值数据预算下，提高中期训练数据的利用效率并降低目标域损失。\n- 提醒训练实践者，脱离学习率设置比较数据配比方案可能得到不可迁移甚至相反的结论。"
notes = "标题：Data Schedules and Learning Rate Schedules Should Be Designed Together\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29348\n摘要：Mid-training (MT) is a training phase in LLM training pipelines that continues pretraining (PT) on smaller datasets of higher value. Taking the PT and MT phases as a whole, training usually starts with only PT data and then transitions completely to MT data. In this work, we view this complete transition from PT to MT data as a special case of a broader class of data mixing schedules that specify how the proportions of PT and MT tokens in each batch change over training. Under fixed total training compute and MT token budgets, we ask: Is this complete transition to MT data optimal for minimizing final MT loss? We show that the answer depends on the learning rate (LR) schedule: one data schedule may outperform another under one LR schedule, but changing only the LR schedule can reverse the "

content = "研究指出，仅依据最终任务结果难以解释深度搜索与深度研究智能体的真实可靠性，错误往往产生于中间工作流。作者提出面向中间执行过程的综合诊断方法与分析框架，并建立结构化错误分类体系。该体系将智能体脆弱性划分为系统级错误和任务级错误两个主要维度。通过分析逐步执行轨迹及智能体—环境交互，研究揭示不同错误如何累积并影响整体表现，同时给出提升鲁棒性、推理能力和可靠性的设计建议。"
purpose = "- 为深度研究智能体建立从最终结果评测转向过程级诊断的分析范式。\n- 可用于定位长程、多步骤任务中的故障来源及错误传播路径。\n- 错误分类体系可指导智能体执行框架、推理流程和环境交互机制的改进。\n- 为轨迹审计、过程评估及智能体可靠性基准设计提供参考。"
notes = "标题：Benchmarking and Error Analysis of Deep Search and Deep Research LLM Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29346\n摘要：As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve into autonomous Deep Search and Deep Research Agents capable of executing long-horizon, multi-step tasks, evaluating their reliability becomes increasingly critical. However, existing evaluation paradigms predominantly focus on final task outcomes, leaving a significant gap in understanding the intermediate execution traces, where agents are highly susceptible to various errors. To address this critical gap, we introduce a comprehensive methodology and analytical framework designed specifically to diagnose errors in intermediate workflows. Building upon this framework, we establish a structured taxonomy that categorizes these vulnerabilities into two primary dimensions: System-Level Errors and Task-Level Errors. Through a systematic examination of ste"

code = ""

link = ""
content = "Policy Central 是一个已部署的政策审查系统，用于识别警务政策条款间的冲突、冗余、相似或差异关系。系统比较了 Retrieve-Judge 检索后分类流水线与 Screen-Confirm 筛选后确认级联架构，前者适合低成本全库健康检查，后者适合对指定文档进行选择性穷举审计。实验基于某警务部门约 84 份真实政策文件构建的 302 对条款基准，Screen-Confirm 在已知候选对上的总体召回率为 0.795，冲突类别召回率为 0.885。候选发现仍是主要瓶颈：Retrieve-Judge 的端到端召回率降至 0.457，Screen-Confirm 为 0.472，但系统已能以较低成本在数分钟内找回约一半目标关系。"

notes = "标题：Policy Central: Detecting Conflicts, Redundancy, and Similarity in Deployed Police Policy\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29338\n摘要：Policy Central is a deployed system that helps police departments find relationships between sections of their written policy: whether two sections conflict, are redundant, are merely similar, or are different. We describe the system and compare two architectures. Retrieve-Judge, a retrieve-then-classify pipeline, runs as a cheap library-wide health signal; Screen-Confirm, a screen-then-confirm cascade, is a selective audit of the documents a reviewer chooses, checking them exhaustively against one another. On a 302-pair benchmark drawn from one department's ~84 real policy documents, given each pair Screen-Confirm reaches 0.795 aggregate recall and 0.885 on Conflict, so current models judge well. Finding the pair is the hard part: Retrieve-Judge's composed recall falls to 0.457, and Scree"


[[items]]
id = "llm-6"
title = "LLM 智能体能否在上下文中执行贝叶斯推断？"
subtopic = "推理评测"
topics = [
    "推理",
    "模型评估",
    "贝叶斯方法",

    "推理评测",
    "内部一致性",

]
score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究系统评估了 LLM 的上下文贝叶斯信念更新能力，发现其通常违反贝叶斯规则，但结合现代推理能力与代码执行后可显著改善，甚至近似解析不可解的后验分布。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29331"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究构建了从定性认识论案例到定量基准的评估框架，同时考察 LLM 信念更新的内部一致性及其恢复外部给定后验分布的能力。实验表明，LLM 在纯上下文推理中通常无法遵循贝叶斯规则，并会采用非贝叶斯式更新。进一步分析显示，这类更新偏差会影响下游任务表现。引入现代推理能力和代码执行工具后，模型表现大幅提升，甚至能够逼近解析不可解的后验分布，展现出服务科学发现中复杂信念更新的潜力。"
purpose = "- 为评估 LLM 的概率一致性与贝叶斯信念更新能力提供系统框架。\n- 提示科学智能体不应仅依赖自然语言推理，应结合代码执行和数值推断工具。\n- 可用于审计假设生成、实验设计等科学工作流中的信念更新偏差。\n- 为构建具备可靠不确定性推理能力的科研智能体提供设计依据。"
notes = "标题：From Prompts to Posteriors: Can LLM Agents Perform Bayesian Inference in Context?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29331\n摘要：Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied across the scientific discovery pipeline, from synthesizing literature to generating hypotheses to informing experimental design. A unifying principle behind these tasks is the need to continuously update beliefs as evidence accumulates, for which Bayesian inference provides a natural framework. However, existing work assumes that LLMs cannot carry out principled Bayesian belief updating in context, instead relying on simplifications and heuristics. To challenge this assumption, we present a principled evaluation of Bayesian reasoning capabilities in LLMs, analyzing both the model's internal consistency as well as its ability to recover an externally determined posterior. Specifically, we build an evaluation framework spanning qualitati"

summary = "研究发现，单次对话中的内部共识表征并不稳定，但跨对话聚合后会呈现马尔可夫动力学，并与外部共识形成幂律映射。"

content = "研究考察两个 LLM 智能体围绕主观问题展开多轮对话时，立场变化与共识形成的动态过程。线性分类器可从激活中识别内部共识表征，但该表征在单次对话和不同轮次间波动较大，无法可靠对应外部共识。跨对话聚合后，外部共识可由一阶马尔可夫过程良好刻画，聚合的内部表征则通过幂律关系与该过程耦合。模型改变立场后，通常会先在语言行为上表达共识，其内部表征随后才反映相同转变。"
purpose = "- 为多智能体交互中的共识形成提供内部表征与外部行为相结合的分析框架。\n- 表明单条对话级探测结果可能噪声较大，跨对话聚合有助于揭示稳定的群体动力学。\n- 外部表态与内部状态之间的时间滞后可用于分析智能体的真实立场、顺从行为及潜在错误共识。\n- 马尔可夫过程与幂律映射可作为模拟和预测多轮意见演化的简化工具。"
notes = "标题：Seeing the Forest for the Trees: Emergent Agreement from Individual Conversations\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29328\n摘要：As LLM agents interact in the wild and shape each other's decisions, understanding how they converge toward agreement becomes important. Externally, this aggregate view reveals a simple structure: when pooled across conversations, agreeme"

code = ""

content = "S&Q 是为 Parlant 4.0 开发的轻量级首阶段相关性过滤器，用于判断每轮对话应应用哪些行为策略。系统先由 LLM 离线为每项策略生成激活样例和困难负例，再基于其嵌入训练可独立更新的逻辑回归分类器，在线阶段仅需向量点积而无需逐轮调用 LLM。该方法可在保持高召回率的前提下排除至少 30% 的候选策略，将昂贵的下游相关性评估集中到更小的候选集合。基于 9,144 个 LLM 评判标签、254 个 τ²-bench 对话前缀和 36 项航空政策的实验显示，S&Q 在各召回率水平上的精确率均优于 Top-K 与余弦阈值检索。"
purpose = "- 可作为客服智能体中的前置策略路由层，降低多策略逐项调用 LLM 的词元与延迟开销。\n- 将昂贵判断转移至离线样例合成与轻量分类器训练，有利于高并发在线部署。\n- 每项策略使用独立分类器，可在策略新增或调整时局部更新，避免整体重训。\n- 困难负例与高召回率约束为构建低漏检的候选过滤系统提供了实践范式。"
notes = "标题：Signals and Queries: Token-Efficient Agentic Context Discovery\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29326\n摘要：User-facing LLM agents must identify which behavioral policies apply at each turn, but evaluating every policy with an LLM is costly. We introduce Signals and Queries (S&Q), a lightweight first-stage relevance filter developed for Parlant 4.0 that removes at least 30% of candidate policies before downstream evaluation while preserving high recall. For each policy, an LLM generates activating examples and hard negatives offline. An independent logistic classifier trained on their embeddings then predicts relevance using embeddings and vector dot products, with no per-turn LLM calls. This shifts expensive relevance evaluation to a smaller candidate set, while allowing each policy's classifier to be updated independently. Evaluated against 9,144 LLM-judge relevance labels covering 254 $τ^2$-b"

notes = "标题：BrainCodes: A Lightweight EEG Foundation Model over Discrete Neural Codes\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29320\n摘要：Recent electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models increasingly emphasize model scale, potentially limiting their deployment on resource-constrained devices and in everyday environments. The pretraining objective explicitly encourages representations that capture spatial connectivity through channel positional encoding, and sensitivity to narrowband neural oscillations through an auxiliary t"

Specifically, the dynamic encoding mechanism generates adaptive input projections conditioned on physical semantics and observation characteristics, enabling flexibl"

content = "GPSCS 将手工割平面选择启发式推广为可学习且完全可解释的符号策略。遗传编程根据候选割及求解上下文特征演化评分函数，对候选割进行排序；轻量级统计机制则通过检测评分显著下降，自适应决定每轮加入的割数量。该方法在 SCIP 中接受七类合成与真实 MILP 基准测试，评估实例平均比训练实例困难 41 倍。实验中，GPSCS 相比 SCIP 默认策略将平均求解时间缩短 6%，相较神经网络方法及无割基线实现约两倍加速。"

notes = "标题：Interpretable Cutting Plane Selection for Mixed-Integer Linear Programming Using Genetic Programming\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29311\n摘要：Cutting planes (cuts) remove fractional solutions from mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs) without excluding any feasible integer solutions, making them a key ingredient in modern MILP solvers. Building on this, we develop Afterstate World-Model Planning (AWMP), which combines an evolved, belief-conditioned dispatching strategy with short scenario-based rollouts. A surprise-triggered refresh mechanism queries the LLM only "

content = "QSelect将模型压缩作为地面环节，为每个线性模块从兼容的AWQ、GPTQ和AutoRound检查点中选择量化方案，最终合成为可受配置控制的静态低比特模型。默认保真模式无需标签或人工设定阈值，仅在替换使模型相对BF16参考的前向KL散度下降时接受该替换；目标适配模式则结合少量标注数据的答案似然。在三款3B至8B模型的3比特和4比特实验中，组合模型在全部六种设置下均获得低于任一单量化器方案的平均留出集困惑度，最终输出评分也优于表征相似度评分。目标适配模式令两款Llama模型的3比特汇总准确率较最佳单量化器方案提高4.3和4.5个百分点，但实验仍限于地面GPU和通用领域基准，尚未覆盖太空任务及飞行硬件。"
purpose = "- 为内存和存储格式严格受限的太空机器人提供可部署的静态语言模型压缩方案。\n- 表明模块级混合量化器选择可优于整模型统一采用AWQ、GPTQ或AutoRound。\n- 将最终词元分布的KL散度作为行为保真指标，有助于维持已完成地面验证模型的行为一致性。\n- 3比特模型中未量化嵌入层与输出层仍占打包产物的43%至46%，提示后续优化应重点处理这些组件。\n- 实际飞行采用前仍需在太空领域任务、辐射与算力约束下开展硬件在环验证。"
notes = "标题：Compressing Onboard Language Models for Space Robots under a Behavioral-Fidelity Requirement\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29302\n摘要：Language models are being explored as the interface through which space robots interpret operator commands and select among verified skills. Running them onboard calls for low-bit compression, and flight use adds requirements that generic post-training quantization leaves implicit: a fixed memory budget and storage format, a static artifact under configuration control, and behavior close to the full-precision model validated on the ground. We treat compression as a ground-segment step and study QSelect, which composes one static low-bit model from compatible AWQ, GPTQ, and AutoRound checkpoints by assigning each linear module to one quantizer family and scoring every substitution at the final next-token distribution. The default fidelity mode needs no labels or tuned thresholds and accepts"

score = 2

summary = "FrameBench-HPS 评测发现，相同概率采用收益或损失框架表达时，七种 LLM 有 32.4% 的完整测试组改变建议类别，而概率规范化可显著缓解这种不一致。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29294"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "- 作者提出 FrameBench-HPS，包含来自医疗、金融、教育与职业、法律及消费者权益领域的 240 个高风险场景，共构造 2,880 条受控提示。\n- 在七种 LLM 上，互补的收益—损失表述使 32.4% 的完整测试组发生建议类别变化，其中多数表现为不确定性变化，而非建议方向直接反转。\n- 人工评估显示，框架变化也会影响用户感知到的风险程度与建议直接性；该效应在对话式改写下依然存在，并超过重复生成产生的普通波动。\n- 将不同表述中的概率统一为规范形式可显著降低框架敏感性，表明概率表示是改善高风险决策支持一致性的可操作切入点。"
purpose = "- 可用于审计医疗、金融和法律等高风险决策支持系统对等价概率表述的稳健性。\n- 提示设计与评测不应只检查最终建议是否正确，还应检测框架变化引发的不确定性和风险感知漂移。\n- 可在输入预处理阶段引入概率规范化，以降低收益—损失框架导致的建议不一致。\n- FrameBench-HPS 可作为研究提示敏感性、认知偏差和决策可靠性的受控评测基准。"
notes = "标题：Same Probabilities, Different Advice: Gain-Loss Framing Effects in High-Stakes LLM Decision Support\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29294\n摘要：Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support consequential decisions, yet their reliability under equivalent representations of uncertainty remains unclear. Existing work has documented prompt sensitivity, cognitive biases, and confidence miscalibration, but it is still unknown whether high-stakes advice changes when the same probability is expressed through complementary gain and loss frames. We introduce FrameBench-HPS, a controlled benchmark of 2,880 prompts derived from 240 high-stakes scenarios across healthcare, finance, education/career, and legal/consumer-rights domains. Across seven LLMs, gain-loss framing changes the recommendation category in 32.4% of complete groups, with most changes involving uncertainty rather than direct reversals. Human evaluation confirms"

notes = "标题：ARCANA: A Benchmark for Abstraction and Analogical Reasoning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29271\n摘要：Analogical reasoning is widely considered a fundamental component of human intelligence. Few benchmarks are designed to explicitly test far analogical reasoning - reasoning between domains that are superficially dissimilar yet share deep relational or structural similarities. In this work, we introduce an analogical reasoning benchmark called Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus of ANAlogy (ARCANA). The tasks of ARCANA require performing far analogical mappings from real-world objects or physical phenomena to abstract grid-based transformations, different from the well-known ARC-AGI benchmark which primarily emphasized innate human priors. We validate ARCANA through a human study, demonstrating that humans solve these tasks reliably. In contrast, evaluations across a range of large language mo"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "HMDLM 为词元位置引入层次优先级和分层噪声调度，在保持全并行解码的同时提升语言生成困惑度与数独多步推理准确率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29255"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "HMDLM 为每个词元位置附加训练数据中可获得的层次级别，用于表达标题、章节或易确定位置相对其他位置的生成优先级。生成被分解为两个阶段：先预测被掩码位置的层次分布，再基于该分布预测词元，并为不同层次配置独立噪声调度。该设计能够软性、可控地优先揭示高优先级位置，同时保持完全并行解码，并重新推导了相应的前向过程、反向过程与 NELBO。在 LM1B 和 WikiHow 上，其困惑度优于 ADLM、MDLM 等扩散基线；在数独任务上，解题准确率最高获得 8.6 个百分点的绝对提升。"
purpose = "- 为具有天然位置依赖关系的文本生成、结构化生成与组合推理任务提供显式优先级建模机制。\n- 说明位置相关的分层噪声调度可以在不牺牲并行解码能力的情况下改善生成质量。\n- 可用于探索文档结构、程序结构或约束满足问题中的粗到细生成与可控揭示顺序。"
notes = "标题：Hierarchical Masked Diffusion Language Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29255\n摘要：Masked diffusion language models have emerged as an alternative to autoregressive models, enabling parallel token generation. However, standard masked diffusion language models use a shared noise schedule across positions without explicitly encoding which positions should be resolved first. Many tasks exhibit a natural priority among positions: document titles and section headings can guide the generation of body text, while Sudoku cells determined directly from the initial clues can help resolve more difficult cells. To incorporate this notion of priority (or hierarchy) among token positions, we introduce the Hierarchical Masked Diffusion Language Model (HMDLM), which augments each token position with a hierarchy level available as part of the training data, and requires zero or minimal a"

content = "MOSAIC包含923个问题，每段以人物设定为基础的交互历史横跨11至24个会话，并同时覆盖文本、图像、视频和音频。基准有针对性地构造空间、时间和韵律信号，而非仅将多媒体作为装饰性输入。其诊断协议固定问题、读取器、提示和计算预算，分别干预记忆的表征、检索与交付环节，从而分解端到端得分。实验表明，表征损失具有明显的模态依赖性，即使提供黄金证据，残余读取错误仍占主导；同时，系统在不可回答样本上的拒答能力依然较弱。"
purpose = "- 用于系统评估全模态对话智能体跨会话保存和调用多模态信息的能力。\n- 将总体准确率拆解为写入表征、证据检索、上下文交付和证据读取等失效来源，便于开展故障归因。\n- 提示记忆系统优化不能只关注检索器，还需改进多模态表征、证据读取及拒答校准。\n- 可作为长期记忆架构、跨模态推理和置信度控制方案的对比评测基准。"
notes = "标题：MOSAIC: An Omni-Modal Benchmark for Long-Term Memory and Cross-Modal Reasoning in Conversational Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29249\n摘要：Omni-modal assistants must remember not only what users say, but also what they show and convey. Existing long-term memory benchmarks are mostly text-and-image or report a single end-to-end score, so they cannot locate whether evidence is lost when it is represented, retrieved, delivered, or read. We introduce MOSAIC, a benchmark of 923 questions over persona-grounded histories of 11-24 sessions spanning text, image, video, and audio. MOSAIC constructs targeted spatial, temporal, and prosodic signals rather than decorative media, with the strongest causal validation for spatial information and selector-level validation for annotated prosody, and pairs them with a matched diagnostic protocol that freezes the question, reader, prompt, and budget while intervening on representation, retrieval"

content = "CrashGym构建了确定性、可回放的限价订单簿模拟器，要求智能体在冲击触发市场崩盘前选择去杠杆干预或流动性支持。智能体可先在实验室市场中查询干预实验，再在独立随机种子的目标市场中提交最终决策。模拟器会在相同目标重复实验上执行全部可行干预，由此直接计算相对最佳方案的遗憾值，无需人工或评判模型打分。前瞻性实验显示，实验室访问将Luna部署的平均遗憾值从8.878降至3.785，降幅为57.4%，但内生演化策略组合相较固定组合尚未显示明确优势。"

notes = "标题：CrashGym: Measuring the Decision Value of Experimentation for LLM Agents in Counterfactual Market Crashes\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29245\n摘要：Language-model agents are increasingly asked to run experiments, yet benchmarks rarely measure whether those experiments improve the decisions they are meant to inform. Before committing, the agent may query intervention experiments in a laboratory realization of the market. The s"

link = ""
content = "研究固定 FLAME 在 MIMIC-III 上冻结分类器产生的药物评分、输出规模与药物相互作用（DDI）约束，单独考察解码策略的影响。精确全局解码器通过混合整数线性规划，联合选择总评分最高且满足 DDI 安全约束的药物集合。实验表明，该方法的 Jaccard 相似度持续优于贪心修复、度感知修复以及加入穷举 1-swap 搜索的局部基线，且约束越严格，优势越明显。在严格设置下，全局解码能保留更多真正例药物并移除更多假正例，说明药物推荐的最终质量不仅取决于表征学习，也取决于评分到安全集合的转换方式。"
purpose = "- 为药物推荐系统提供可验证的集合级安全约束解码方案。\n- 说明逐对删除冲突药物的局部修复可能引入可避免的预测误差。\n- 启示临床生成系统在冻结基础预测器后，仍可通过全局组合优化改善安全性与准确率权衡。\n- 可作为比较启发式解码与精确约束优化方法的评测范式。"
notes = "标题：From Scores to Safe Sets: Exact Safety--Constrained Decoding for Medication Recommendation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29228\n摘要：Recent generative medication recommendation systems ultimately produce sets of drugs, but drug--drug interaction (DDI) constraints depend on the set as a whole. A natural post-hoc approach is to first predict medications and then locally repair interacting pairs by removing one drug at a time. We ask whether this local repair step itself introduces avoidable error. Using fixed medication scores from FLAME's frozen classifier on MIMIC-III, we compare increasingly strong local repair strategies with an exact global decoder while holding the scores, output size, and DDI constraint fixed. The global decoder jointly selects the highest-scoring feasible medication set using mixed-integer linear programming. Across validation and test splits, global decoding consistently achieves higher Jaccard s"

code = ""

content = "DiagSafe 为每条提示配置细粒度评测量规，分别衡量具体危害与指令完成度，并通过明确豁免条件减少安全误报。配套英中双语基准采用包含 84 个标签的安全分类体系，由覆盖直接有害请求、模板化越狱及多轮对话的 DiagBase，以及将有害意图嵌入复杂指令任务的 DiagAttack 组成。实验中，该框架与人工标注的一致率达到 95.34%，高于基线的 69%–87%，不同自动评判器间的 Cohen’s κ 达到 0.864。诊断结果还显示，不同领先模型具有差异化安全弱点，且在中文和多轮场景中尤其容易暴露风险。"
purpose = "- 为安全审计提供比“安全/不安全”二元标签更细致、可解释的失效诊断。\n- 将安全性与有用性分开评估，避免把正常拒答或合理回答简单归入同一结果。\n- 可用于比较自动评判器的一致性，并校准 LLM-as-a-judge 安全评测流程。\n- 提示安全对齐与红队测试应重点覆盖中文、多轮交互及复杂指令攻击。"
notes = "标题：Beyond Binary Labels: Diagnostic Evaluation of LLM Safety with Prompt-Level Rubrics\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29225\n摘要：Current LLM safety benchmarks predominantly rely on binary safe or unsafe labels, lacking explanatory depth and suffering from low consistency across automated judges. To address this, we introduce DiagSafe, a diagnostic evaluation framework that moves beyond binary classification by employing prompt-level rubrics. Grounded in globally applicable AI safety policies, each rubric provides explicit criteria along two dimensions: safety, which targets concrete harms with specific exemptions to suppress false positives, and usefulness, which independently evaluates instruction fulfillment. To ensure a robust and realistic evaluation, we construct a bilingual (English-Chinese) benchmark grounded in a comprehensive 84-label safety taxonomy, consisting of two subsets: DiagBase, which covers single"

notes = "标题：PerSPL: Personalized Semi-supervised Preference Learning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29219\n摘要：Personalized reward models require user-specific preference comparisons, but collecting such feedback is costly and provides only limited supervision for each user. Directly learning from these responses is difficult because pairwise objectives constrain relative scores within a prompt, whereas pseudo-labeling individual responses requires scores that are comparable across prompts for the same user. We propose \\textbf{PerSPL} (Personalized Semi-supervised Preference Learning), a backbone-agnostic framework that uses pair-mean score centering to stabilize this cross-prompt score coordinate and iteratively turns unlabeled re"

content = "论文将购物智能体的决策从“商品是否符合偏好”扩展为三级授权判断：不购买、先确认、自动购买。授权边界由商品信息充分性、交易可逆性、价格相对用户常态及紧迫性共同决定，而非仅取决于偏好匹配。对16个指令微调模型的评测显示，模型即使在匹配度低或证据不足时仍可能直接购买，显著偏离15名人工标注者给出的授权参考。个体之间的许可边界虽有差异，但具有可预测结构；使用个人30至40条标签训练的浅层学习器取得0.79的中位预测表现，超过全部提示与模型组合，但该边界难以跨领域迁移。作者计划在论文接收后发布反事实基准、人工授权参考及标注协议。"
purpose = "- 为购物智能体建立区别于偏好推荐的授权安全评测框架，重点检测自主交易中的越权行为。\n- 提示自动购买系统应显式建模证据充分性、价格、可逆性和紧迫性，并在边界不清时请求用户确认。\n- 表明个性化授权边界可通过少量用户标签学习，但不宜假设其能够直接跨商品领域迁移。\n- 可用于设计动作授权策略、人在回路机制及面向代理商业场景的用户保护措施。"
notes = "标题：Preference Is Not Permission: Personalizing the Auto-Purchase Line in Agentic Commerce\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29216\n摘要：Personalized shopping benchmarks ask which item fits the user; we ask how far an agent may commit on that item, and pose learning the individual's auto-purchase line as a task. The boundary is evidence-conditioned and three-valued: whether committing is appropriate depends on the sufficiency of product information (warrant), reversibility, price against the user's norm, and urgency, not only on fit; and the natural output is don't buy, confirm first, or buy automatically. Across sixteen instruction-tuned models, autonomous commitment is misaligned with a human authorization reference in which fifteen annotators, role-playing the delegating principal from a given profile, choose the furthest action they would allow without confirmation: models commit even when fit is low and when their own "

content = "NorRegel包含916个从Lovdata开放数据机械抽取的问题，覆盖挪威法律、法规和议会决定中的金额、百分比、国民保险基本金额倍数及年龄限制。每道题均指定日期并附有条款数值的历史记录，可将回答标注为正确、过时、错误或不可解释。第二次预注册实验涵盖8个模型，汇总主检验显示模型知识截止日期之后生效的数值更易引发过时回答，但关键敏感性分析未能复现这一结果，因此证据并不稳健。实验还发现部分模型大量生成不可解释回答，而新挪威语与书面挪威语之间的正确率差异未得出明确结论。"

notes = "标题：norregel: a preregistered benchmark of dated numeric values in Norwegian law\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29207\n摘要：norregel asks language models for numeric values in Norwegian statutes, regulations and parliamentary decisions (NOK amounts, percentages, multiples of the National Insurance basic amount (G) and age limits), each at a stated date. In the first run (five models, listed publicly only after local analysis), H1, that outdated answers are more frequent for values that took effect after a model's cutoff, could not be tested in substance, and H2 and H3 were inconclusive; Opus 5.5 and Sonnet 4.6 answered 46.7% and 40.2% of the 880 prima"

notes = "标题：Reasoning-Search-Augmented LLMs: An Operational Taxonomy, Benchmark Analysis and Controlled Evidence Synthesis\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29189\n摘要：Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate impressive emergent reasoning capabilities, yet they remain constrained by fixed model parameters and finite context windows. Even when augmented with static retrieval, they can produce statements that are unsupported by, or even contradictory to, retrieved evidence. This leads to hallucinations, difficulty incorporating up-to-date information, and failures to correctly interpret or reconcile sources. Recent work addresses these limitations by tightly coupling step-by-step reasoning with interactive search, treating retrieval as an action within an iterative agentic loop rather than a one-shot preprocessing step. This study reviews frameworks that implement such reasoning-search loops by interleaving planning, querying, retrieval, integration, verif"

score = 2

summary = "OrbitWeaver通过阶段感知检查点与依赖感知动态恢复，使星载视觉语言模型在断电、重启等多次中断后保留推理进度并高效续算。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29183"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "OrbitWeaver面向低地球轨道卫星上的视觉语言模型推理，解决功耗、散热、故障重启及载荷竞争导致的执行中断问题。框架根据视觉编码、语言模型预填充和解码阶段的状态生命周期及计算特征选择检查点边界，权衡存储写入与重复计算。其依赖感知恢复机制沿网络层和词元分块维度调度部分状态恢复，并将检查点及权重读取与重新计算重叠。卫星载荷实验显示，其端到端速度最高提升4.07倍、SSD写入量最高降低3.56倍；两颗卫星上的72小时部署验证了跨多次强制关机续算的可行性。"
purpose = "- 为易受供电与热约束影响的端侧推理任务提供跨重启状态持久化方案。\n- 展示按推理阶段设计检查点粒度，可在恢复速度、重复计算和存储寿命之间取得平衡。\n- 可启发长程智能体及边缘多模态系统构建具备故障恢复能力的执行运行时。\n- 证明消费级星载硬件能够在真实遥测与反复关机场景下可靠续接VLM推理。"
notes = "标题：Resuming Vision-Language Inference across Onboard Interruptions\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29183\n摘要：Executing vision-language models (VLMs) aboard low-Earth orbit satellites enables flexible processing of observation data without first downlinking it to the ground. On commercial off-the-shelf payloads, however, a request may span multiple execution windows: power and thermal constraints, fault-triggered reboots, and competing payload demands can interrupt inference before it completes. Each shutdown discards volatile activations and KV caches, forcing an unprotected request to repeat work after restart. We present OrbitWeaver, a framework that preserves VLM inference progress across onboard interruptions through coordinated checkpointing and recovery. Its phase-aware checkpointing selects recovery boundaries according to the state lifetimes and computational characteristics of vision enc"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29171"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "TrainBridge 针对昇腾 NPU 训练程序中算子融合不充分、计算碎片化和执行开销偏高等问题，引入 GPU 到 NPU 的优化知识迁移。系统将成熟 GPU 编译栈产生的高效执行结果作为任务特定参考，从中提取优化意图并指导 NPU 程序重写。重写后的程序通过迭代性能分析继续优化，以减少传统性能调优所需的高成本试错。实验覆盖 LLM 预训练、图像分类和视觉语言训练等四类工作负载，平均获得 1.7 倍加速；在 27 项 Modded-NanoGPT speedrun 记录上平均加速接近 2.0 倍。"
purpose = "- 为不同加速器之间迁移编译优化经验提供可自动化的智能体方案。\n- 可利用成熟 GPU 软件栈弥补 NPU 编译优化能力不足，降低算子融合与程序重写的人工成本。\n- 表明执行剖析证据可作为硬件后端优化智能体的反馈信号，适用于异构训练系统调优。\n- 为构建跨硬件、闭环迭代的训练性能优化工具链提供参考。"
notes = "标题：TrainBridge: Agentic NPU Training Optimization Guided by GPU Compilation Evidence\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29171\n摘要：We study how to improve training efficiency on Ascend NPUs. In the NPU training programs we study, framework-level compilation optimizations such as operator fusion are only partially applied, leaving fragmented computation and avoidable execution overheads. Profiling-guided tuning can address these inefficiencies, but it often requires costly trial and error. To make training on NPUs more efficient, we propose TrainBridge, an agentic approach for transferring GPU optimization guidance to NPU training. TrainBridge uses the highly optimized execution produced by mature GPU compilation stacks as a task-specific reference for NPU tuning. It extracts optimization intents from the GPU training execution to guide NPU program rewrites and further refines the rewritten program through iterative pr"

We quantify this with nuisance orthogonality; orthogonality of the feature under visual variation outside the empirical in-distribution manifold. Across various image and video backbones, we show that a higher orthogonality from the representational prior is associated with stronger robustness to visual variations after WAM trainin"

Quality-based selection raises quality passes from 12 to 19 of 20 for Cheff-identified images and from 8 to 17 for SynthCheX. We find no evidence that selection improves passage thro"

content = "ORSMT面向自然语言优化问题生成流程，验证书面数学模型、求解器代码和结果报告之间的语义一致性，而非仅比较表面形式。系统对齐可观测决策、投影辅助变量，并通过精确SMT查询证明或反驳可行域及目标函数之间的关系，同时将反例定位到相关约束行或变量域。它还可依据一个制品重建另一个制品，并要求候选修复通过既定关系检查后才能采用。在3,034个问题和六种生成器规模上，受保护选择将整体模型—代码匹配率从43.2%提高至88.2%，对被反驳样本进行一轮源文本修订与精确重建后，84.4%的样本恢复匹配。"
purpose = "- 为LLM自动生成优化模型建立可证明的跨制品语义验收机制，避免仅凭运行结果或目标值判断正确性。\n- 利用SMT反例定位具体约束和变量域，可支持优化建模代码的自动诊断与定向修复。\n- “验证后采用”的候选选择策略可作为生成式编程系统的安全门，降低错误修复被直接写入下游流程的风险。\n- 对辅助变量进行投影并比较可行域关系，为验证形式不同但语义等价的优化模型提供通用思路。"
notes = "标题：ORSMT: Unleashing the Potential of Satisfiability Modulo Theories for Semantic Verification and Repair in LLM-Based Optimization\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29145\n摘要：Large language models increasingly automate optimization modeling from natural language problems, producing written mathematics, solver code and a returned report. Verifying one optimization problem across these artifacts requires semantic comparison: unequal models can attain the same objective, equivalent models can use different auxiliary variables, and mutually consistent artifacts can omit the same source requirement. ORSMT provides a typed semantic interface and exact SMT relation queries. It aligns observable decisions, projects auxiliary variables, proves or refutes feasible region and objective relations, localizes refutations to implicated rows or domains and links source texts when available. It then reconstructs one artifact from another, and each adopted candidate passes the r"

score = 2

summary = "VeeReview 以 V 模型的四类构建—验证配对衡量 Pull Request 的验证证据，但其低校准度和弱标注一致性表明，当前工具尚不足以可靠比较智能体与人类的验证实践。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29143"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "VeeReview 结合代码仓库确定性信号、语言模型评判以及基于 diff 和仓库树的规则，对四类构建—验证配对进行分类。作者先在两名评审者标注的 25 个 Pull Request 参考集上评估该工具，再将其用于来自 741 个仓库的 1,454 组智能体与人类 PR。结果显示，智能体 PR 在四类配对上的“仅构建”比例均较低，但 Architecture–Integration 差异不显著，控制关联 issue、测试交付和改动规模后，组间差异缩小 34%～60%。该工具与裁决标签的一致率仅为 4%～60%，未达到预注册的 80% 标准，且评审者间一致性较弱（κ=0.154），说明语言模型过程度量可能更多反映 PR 的呈现方式，而非实际验证工作。"
purpose = "- 为智能体生成代码的 PR 审查提供面向验证证据的结构化测量框架。\n- 提醒评测设计者不要直接以语言模型评判结果推断真实的软件验证实践，应引入机械可判定指标。\n- 在比较智能体与人类开发行为前，应建立经过验证的人工参考集并检查标注一致性与构念效度。\n- 可用于设计更可靠的代码智能体评测、PR 验收测试和过程审计工具。"
notes = "标题：VeeReview: Measuring Build-Verify Pairs in Agent-Authored Pull Requests from a V-Model Perspective\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29143\n摘要：Context: Reviewing a pull request requires verification evidence that addresses the proposed change. Coding agent benchmarks and studies of pull request outcomes do not directly assess this relationship. Objective: We test whether the V-Model's build and verify pairs can measure verification evidence in pull requests and support comparisons between agent-authored and human-authored changes. Method: VeeReview classifies four build and verify pairs using repository signals and a language-model judge. Three pairs use model judgements constrained by deterministic signals, and a rule over the diff and repository tree decides Module Design-Unit Test. We evaluated VeeReview against a 25-pull request reference set labelled by two raters and applied it to 1,454 matched pairs of agent and human pull"

notes = "标题：Steering Black-Box Large Language Model Agents with Pitfall Anticipation World Model\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29139\n摘要：Frontier black-box Large Language Model (LLM) agents are proficient at long-horizon reasoning and precise action generation. A world model offers a natural remedy, providing the agent with environment specific knowledge by foreseeing how the environment will respond to the action it is about to take. Prior world models for LLM agents mainly predict next observations at each step, which is costly and often uninformative because individual observations provide limited task-relevant guidance and not every step requires call for foresight. This raises a key question: what a world model should predict and when should it be invoked for agentic tasks? Our pilot study "

summary = "GRAFT通过在连续扩散语言模型尚未确定输出时提前选定易被平均机制淘汰的罕见词，并基于约束重新生成序列，无需训练即可恢复词汇长尾分布。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29136"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "连续扩散语言模型仅在最终阶段将嵌入转为文本，中间步骤不进行离散词采样，这虽然提升了采样效率，却造成词汇长尾坍缩。实验显示，ELF生成最罕见词的频率仅为人类文本的0.30倍，而且该差距不会随参数规模从105M扩大至652M而消失。作者将问题归因于连续扩散过程中对候选词表示求平均，使罕见候选容易被竞争位置上的常见词覆盖。GRAFT在模型仍未确定时提交罕见词约束，再固定这些选择从头生成序列，使多个ELF规模及LangFlow的罕见词占比恢复至人类水平的0.82至1.10倍。"
purpose = "- 揭示连续扩散语言模型取消逐步离散化所带来的词汇长尾坍缩问题。\n- 提供一种无需重新训练、可直接作用于冻结模型的词汇分布校正方案。\n- 说明高效并行生成不仅需要关注质量与采样步数，还应评估罕见词覆盖和整体词频分布。\n- 为连续生成模型设计显式候选选择或离散承诺接口提供依据。"
notes = "标题：GRAFT: Restoring the Missing Sampling Interface in Continuous Diffusion Language Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29136\n摘要：Continuous diffusion language models generate a whole sequence in parallel, never drawing a single word at any intermediate step; only the final step turns embeddings into text. Models such as ELF now report autoregressive-level quality at a fraction of the sampling steps of discrete-token diffusion models, and this design principle -- no step-wise discretization -- is what their own ablations credit for the gain. We show that this same principle carries a hidden cost: a collapse of the lexical long tail. Measured against held-out human text, ELF's output matches the human rate on common words almost exactly, yet its rarest words appear at only 0.30x the human rate, a gap that does not close from 105M to 652M parameters and that no sampler setting these models expose can reach. A same-size"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究通过对模型内部表征实施动态干预，在工具调用和长上下文等真实场景中低成本抑制奖励作弊，同时尽量保持模型连贯性与能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29129"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究面向包含工具调用和长上下文的现实生产场景，探索奖励作弊的监测与缓解机制。作者分别从标注轨迹的均值差、梯度优化以及模型引用反奖励作弊规范时的激活中提取引导方向。动态干预仅在必要时调整模型内部表征，相比向残差流统一加入引导向量，扰动更小且编辑更精确。实验表明，该方法能显著降低奖励作弊，对模型连贯性和能力影响有限，并且从编程环境提取的引导向量可泛化到其他领域。"
purpose = "- 为生产系统中的奖励作弊提供低成本、可动态启用的潜空间控制方案。\n- 说明表征干预可以兼顾安全行为修正与基础能力保持，减少统一强干预带来的性能损失。\n- 展示跨领域引导方向的迁移能力，为通用奖励作弊监测器和运行时安全干预机制提供依据。"
notes = "标题：Mitigating Reward Hacking in Realistic Scenarios Through Representation Engineering\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29129\n摘要：Reward hacking is a major problem in current production machine learning systems, resulting in costly and dangerous failures. Here we study how to monitor for and mitigate reward hacking in realistic settings, including tool calls and long contexts. We apply a dynamic intervention to steering directions obtained by three different methods: difference-in-means vectors from labeled rollouts, gradient optimization-based vectors, and vectors extracted when models reference an anti-reward-hacking model spec. This dynamic intervention perturbs model representations less than uniform steering vector addition to the residual stream, allowing for more precise edits. We find that we are able to cheaply and significantly reduce reward hacking without correspondingly significant costs to model coheren"

score = 2

summary = "该旅游规划器以Z数联合表示出行时间及证据可靠性，并通过混合目标与自适应大邻域搜索生成兼顾碳排放、耗时和可信度的单日行程。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29120"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究从六个出发时段的路线服务观测和平台评论数量中直接构造Z数，同时表达数值的模糊约束及其证据可靠性。系统以评分、碳排放、出行时间和可靠性构成混合损失，并使用自适应大邻域搜索生成带有逐路段可靠性标注的单日行程。五座城市共150次配对实验显示，相较于均值基线，行程碳排放下降19.3%，总出行时间下降11.5%。由于部分城市的评论数为估计值且实验仅采用一个基线，结果主要证明了方案的可行性与潜在改进方向，而非普遍优势。"
purpose = "- 为存在时段波动和证据质量差异的旅游路线规划提供显式不确定性建模方案。\n- 展示如何将碳排放、时间、用户评分和证据可靠性纳入统一的多目标优化过程。\n- 逐路段可靠性标注可增强行程推荐的透明度，并支持用户进行风险感知决策。\n- 后续可引入更多基线、真实评论数据及动态交通场景，以验证跨城市泛化能力。"
notes = "标题：An Eco-Friendly Tourism Trip Planner Driven by Z-Number Uncertainty: Evidence from Baku, Dubai, Abu Dhabi, Tashkent, and Istanbul\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29120\n摘要：Deployed tourism trip planners ingest travel times and ratings as exact numbers, although routing services return travel times that vary with the hour of departure and a platform rating may rest on a handful of reviews or on tens of thousands. We present an eco-friendly trip planner that represents both quantities as Z-numbers, which pair a fuzzy restriction on a value with a fuzzy reliability on the evidence behind it, and that constructs them directly from routing-service observations at six departure hours and from platform review counts rather than from linguistic elicitation. A hybrid loss combines score, carbon, travel-time, and reliability terms, and an adaptive large neighborhood search returns single-day itineraries with per-leg reliability annotations through a conversational fro"

id = "mixagent"
title = "MixAgent：智能体音乐后期制作与混音修复"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "MixAgent以参考歌曲指导可解释、可编辑的混音规划，并利用MixCritic数据集及偏好优化训练修复批评器，在MedleyDB上将初始混音误差降低19%。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29093"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "MixAgent模拟音频工程师的工作流，由分组智能体将鼓等相关输入音轨路由至子组，再由规划智能体结合检索示例，为音轨、子组和完整混音选择并排序音量控制、混响等效果。系统通过检测器定位待修复问题，并由批评器提出从单条音轨到整体混音的数值参数调整。作者构建了包含45,657条修复批评的MixCritic数据集，并采用监督微调与基于渲染音频结果的偏好优化训练批评器。在MedleyDB上，学习式修复相对初始混音降低19%的误差，偏好优化较单独监督训练再降低8.4%，人工听测质量也优于Qwen3-Omni、Gemini 3.8 Flash和GPT-4o-audio。"
purpose = "- 展示智能体如何将专业音频工程流程拆解为分组、效果规划、问题检测和参数修复等可解释模块。\n- 表明直接提示多模态模型反复试听和修改可能降低混音质量，可靠的检测与数值化修复机制更为关键。\n- MixCritic可为音频编辑批评器、混音参数优化及偏好学习提供规模化训练数据范式。\n- 检索专业示例能够改善音频效果选择，可用于构建人在回路、可编辑的音乐制作助手。"
notes = "标题：Agentic Music Post-Production\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29093\n摘要：We introduce MixAgent, an agentic pipeline for music post-production that follows the workflow of audio engineers to build interpretable and editable mixing plans guided by a reference song. A grouping agent first routes related input tracks, such as drum recordings, into subgroups. A planning agent then uses retrieved examples to choose and order audio effects, such as volume control and reverb, for tracks, subgroups, and the full mix. To refine the mix, a detector identifies what needs correction, and a critic proposes numerical parameter adjustments from tracks to the full mix. In our experiments, prompting recent multimodal models to listen and revise often worsens the mix, making reliable correction a central challenge. We therefore build MixCritic, a dataset of 45,657 repair critique"

source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文提出多语言推理轨迹分歧评测框架，发现即使最终答案相同，不同语言生成的推理结构与步骤顺序仍可能显著不一致。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29092"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文将可见推理轨迹建模为结构化序列，从结构相似性、顺序一致性和整体相似性三个维度衡量跨语言一致程度，并针对语言对进行校准。研究覆盖三个推理基准、七种语言和六个前沿模型。实验显示，整段响应嵌入的余弦相似度会系统性高估一致性：整体相似度位于最高四分位的样本中，有 52.1%～67.3% 未进入结构相似度的最高四分位。双语盲评表明，人类判断更符合结构相似性指标；同语言对照实验则进一步说明，这类轨迹分歧具有语言特异性。"
purpose = "- 为多语言推理系统提供比答案一致率和整段语义相似度更细粒度的评测方法。\n- 提醒安全审计、教育及验证场景关注不同语言用户是否获得等价的推理证据。\n- 可用于识别“答案相同但理由不同”的隐性可靠性风险，并辅助跨语言部署前的过程评估。\n- 说明推理轨迹的结构与步骤顺序应成为多语言模型评测的重要维度。"
notes = "标题：Same Answer, Different Reasons: Trace-Level Divergence in Multilingual Large Language Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29092\n摘要：Multilingual large language models are increasingly deployed in settings where visible reasoning traces support verification, education, and safety auditing, yet standard evaluations typically measure only final-answer agreement or holistic response similarity. As a result, they establish outcome consistency without determining whether users across languages receive comparable supporting evidence. We introduce a trace-level divergence framework that compares visible reasoning traces as structured sequences, decomposing agreement into structural similarity, order consistency, and holistic similarity, with pair-specific calibration to enable comparison across languages. Across three reasoning benchmarks, seven languages, and six frontier LLMs, the natural baseline---embedding each whole resp"

id = "item-193"
title = "物理参数偏移下的因果世界模型"
subtopic = "世界模型"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究通过干预学习的依赖掩码与显式物理机制建模，提高世界模型在机器人质量参数分布外变化下的预测能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29088"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究将因果世界模型拆分为输入依赖关系与作用机制两部分，考察其在物理参数超出训练分布时的泛化能力。合成基准中，基于配对干预得到的冻结依赖掩码将分布外 RMSE 从 1.769 降至 0.697，接近生成器真值掩码的 0.694。在 CausalWorld 机器人实验中，作者构建手指局部世界模型，让连杆质量通过显式仿射系数进入惯性项和动力学偏置项，并以学习残差刻画接触效应。采用已知 PD 控制器的模型在全部十个任务—随机种子组合上均获提升，150 步滚动预测的中位关节 R² 从 0.609 提升至 0.727。结果表明，分布外物理参数是否有助于预测，关键取决于参数被注入动力学机制的方式，而非是否简单提供该参数。"
purpose = "- 为具身世界模型应对质量、摩擦等物理参数偏移提供因果结构设计思路。\n- 说明显式编码惯性、动力学偏置和控制器机制，可比直接拼接参数获得更可靠的分布外泛化。\n- 配对干预学习的依赖掩码可用于抑制捷径特征，提升动力学预测的因果稳健性。\n- 可作为机器人仿真到现实迁移及变化物理条件下长时预测的建模参考。"
notes = "标题：Causal world models under physical parameter shifts\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29088\n摘要：World models predict how embodied systems evolve under actions, but their accuracy can degrade when physical conditions move outside the training distribution. We study two components of causal modeling: which inputs a predictor may use, and how its mechanism computes their effects. In a synthetic benchmark with shortcut features, a frozen dependency mask derived from paired interventions reduces mean out-of-distribution (OOD) RMSE from 1.769 for a fully connected predictor to 0.697, close to the generator-oracle mask (0.694). For robot link-mass extrapolation in CausalWorld, with disjoint training and test mass ranges, we develop finger-local world models in which link masses enter inertia and dynamics-bias terms through explicit affine coefficients, actions act through either a learned t"


[[items]]
id = "item-194"
title = "更优策略反而导致更差的验证器审计"
subtopic = "评测审计"
topics = [
    "过程评估",
    "模型评估",
    "人工智能可靠性",
    "动态基准测试",
    "分布评估",

    "Awesome-RSI",
]
keywords = [
    "评测审计",
    "过程评估",
    "分布评估",
    "验证器",
    "动态基准测试",
]
score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文揭示策略能力提升会改变在线失败样本分布，导致组件验证器的聚合审计失真，甚至反向偏好更差的验证器。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29029"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文研究后训练与测试时计算循环中的“在线策略审计崩塌”：组件验证器在新策略输出上依据整体成功标签接受评估和更新。对于合取目标 $y=F\\wedge M$，随着策略逐渐掌握组件 $F$，失败样本会越来越偏向满足 $F$ 但未满足 $M$ 的案例，使审计指标减少对 $F$ 自身失败的关注。作者推导了可识别集合、完全擦除阈值，以及审计开始偏好更差组件验证器的精确条件，并指出该指标的总体最优解是完整结果预测器而非组件预言机。文本实验和预注册 ECG 实验表明，依据这种审计更新验证器会降低离策略的组件 AUC、将损害迁移至未见生成器，并增加下游约束违规。修复方案是在审计与刷新分布中保留组件自身的失败样本，并采用受控混合审计。"
purpose = "- 提醒后训练和测试时计算系统：策略持续进步并不意味着验证器审计会同步变得更可靠。\n- 设计组件验证器评测时，应保留该组件自身失败样本的支持度，避免动态输出分布掩盖真实能力退化。\n- 可采用受控失败样本混合与离策略评估，降低在线分布变化造成的审计反转。\n- 对包含多个约束的验证器栈，应分别审计各组件目标，而非仅依赖聚合成功标签。"
notes = "标题：Better Policies, Worse Verifier Audits\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29029\n摘要：Verifier stacks increasingly sit inside post-training and test-time compute loops. We study a specific control loop: a component verifier is evaluated on fresh policy outputs against the full success label and refreshed when that aggregate score falls. For a conjunctive target $y = F \\wedge M$, its on-policy AUC is $(1-q)A_{\\mathrm{own}} + qA_{\\mathrm{cross}}$, where $q = \\Pr[F=1 \\mid y=0]$. As the policy masters $F$, $q$ can rise toward one, removing weight from $F$-failures and rewarding information about $M$. We call this on-policy audit collapse. We derive a sharp identified set, a complete-erasure threshold, and the exact point at which the audit can prefer a worse component verifier; its population optimum is the full-outcome predictor, not the component oracle. An exact oracle exhib"

summary = "GenXR-Bench 通过112项专家编写的交互式XR开发任务和真实消费级硬件测试，系统评估编程智能体在动态三维环境中的端到端开发能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29014"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "GenXR-Bench 是面向交互式扩展现实（XR）软件开发的端到端编程智能体评测基准，涵盖24项功能实现、21项缺陷修复和67项应用生成任务。其保留测试结合脚本化与自适应交互，并使用程序化断言和视觉断言检查核心功能及运行时行为。测试套件在消费级XR硬件上对124个候选应用进行验证，与盲审专家判断的一致准确率达到96.5%。对10个模型的评测显示，最强配置的总体 Pass@1 仅为50.9%，主要失误涉及异步传感器生命周期、物理环境落地和具身交互语义。"
purpose = "- 为编程智能体在空间计算与动态三维环境中的能力提供可复现评测框架。\n- 支持同时衡量代码正确性、视觉输出、交互行为和真实硬件运行鲁棒性。\n- 揭示传统代码基准难以覆盖的传感器生命周期、物理 grounding 与具身交互问题。\n- 可用于指导XR编程智能体的训练数据构建、故障分析和工具链改进。"
notes = "标题：GenXR-Bench: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Interactive XR Development\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29014\n摘要：Coding agents are rapidly advancing from single-file generation to long-horizon repository-scale engineering, yet the evaluation of their capabilities to author interactive extended reality (XR) applications remains an open challenge. Unlike traditional software, XR applications operate within dynamic 3D physical environments where runtime robustness depends jointly on continuous user motion, spatial input, and environmental sensing. We introduce GenXR-Bench, the first benchmark for the end-to-end evaluation of coding agents in XR software development. GenXR-Bench contains 112 expert-authored tasks spanning 24 feature implementations, 21 bug repairs, and 67 application generation tasks. Held-out tests combine scripted and adaptive interactions with programmatic and visual assertions to eva"

For challenging cases requiring further adjustments, an LLM-based multi-agent system enables plan refinement through natural language interactions. The system comprises a planning agent that recommends evidence-supported adjustments, a chatbot providi"

summary = "DoobPO 利用 Doob h-变换与条件漂移匹配，在无需计算动作似然的情况下以 PPO、GRPO 等规则微调扩散和流生成策略。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28990"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "DoobPO 面向动作似然难以计算的扩散策略与流策略，提供一种无似然强化学习微调框架。该框架将策略改进与生成式策略学习解耦，先训练轻量级比率模型表示目标策略更新，再通过条件漂移匹配将比率倾斜后的目标策略蒸馏回生成策略。作者证明，实用的条件漂移匹配目标与理想 Doob 漂移匹配目标在梯度上等价，且诱导的扩散过程对应端点约束随机最优控制问题的最小能量解。在 MuJoCo 连续控制基准上，DoobPO 相较多种扩散和流策略基线取得了较强表现。"
purpose = "- 为难以显式计算动作似然的生成式策略提供兼容 PPO、GRPO 的强化学习微调路径。\n- 通过解耦策略改进和生成策略拟合，降低将通用策略优化规则迁移到扩散及流策略的难度。\n- 可用于机器人连续控制、序列决策以及其他采用生成式动作分布的策略学习任务。\n- 理论上的梯度等价性和最小能量解释有助于分析训练目标的正确性与稳定性。"
notes = "标题：Doob-Based Generative Policy Optimization\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28990\n摘要：Diffusion and flow models provide expressive policy classes for robotic and sequential decision-making systems. However, their action likelihoods are generally intractable, limiting the direct use of widely adopted policy optimization methods such as PPO and GRPO. We propose Doob Policy Optimization (DoobPO), a framework that leverages Doob's $h$-transform for likelihood-free reinforcement learning fine-tuning of generative policies. DoobPO separates policy improvement from generative policy learning. It first learns a lightweight ratio model that represents the desired policy update, and then distills the resulting ratio-tilted target policy into the generative policy via conditional drift matching. This formulation applies uniformly to both diffusion and flow policies and supports a broa"

code = ""

content = "AgentPolicyForge面向定制智能体部署中的工具、资源和执行条件策略，解决仅靠提示词难以可靠阻止违规调用的问题。框架基于可执行环境运行工具调用并记录结果，从参数、资源和上下文层面判定合规性，而非仅检查工具身份。作者据此构建PolicyGym，覆盖10类策略，包含698个训练场景和254个独立、经人工审查的测试场景。通过攻击者生成请求、环境验证样本和防护模型迭代微调，最终配置相较单纯策略提示将攻击成功率相对降低40%至44%，同时减少对正常请求的误拒。"
purpose = "- 为智能体工具调用策略提供可执行、可复核的安全评测与训练数据生成流程。\n- 表明策略防护必须检查调用参数、访问资源及执行上下文，不能只依赖工具白名单。\n- 可用于迭代训练独立策略防护模型，并通过持续更新攻击策略档案增强对新型违规请求的适应能力。\n- PolicyGym可作为比较提示约束、自我监管与外置防护机制的统一基准。"
notes = "标题：AgentPolicyForge: From Executable Environments to Policy Benchmarks and Adaptive Guards\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28989\n摘要：Customized agent deployments define policies over tools, resources, and execution conditions. Providing these policies in the task agent's prompt does not reliably prevent violations. Training a guard requires examples of tool calls that comply with or violate those policies. We introduce AgentPolicyForge, a framework that uses executable environments to construct policy benchmarks and iteratively train policy guards. With this framework, we construct PolicyGym, a dataset across ten policy families using 698 scenarios for training data generation and 254 separate held-out scenarios for human-reviewed test sets. Tool calls are executed and their outcomes recorded to help determine whether they comply with the policy. Every violating case uses a permitted tool, requiring checks of arguments,"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "PerMIT 通过有限记忆接口、确认门与 Lean 4 机器验证的完整性定理，防止环境来源内容直接污染智能体持久记忆，并以社区信任机制支持安全的跨智能体知识共享。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28987"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "PerMIT 将用户输入及确认门批准的值视为可信，并通过有限记忆接口控制环境来源内容的释放与使用，同时保留智能体的多轮规划和工具选择能力。作者提出两项经 Lean 4 机器检查的完整性定理，保证环境记忆只有经过确认才能触发敏感操作，且只能通过确认门展示的内容影响智能体行为。在 Trojan Hippo 上，结合自验证或轻量验证器后未观察到攻击成功。系统还将精确内容的确认结果记录为背书，使知识可在社区中积累信任；在 τ²-bench 上，最多 90 个智能体在未推广直接记忆注入内容的情况下构建共享知识库，并提升了新智能体在留出任务上的表现。"
purpose = "- 为持久记忆中的提示注入与知识投毒提供可形式化验证的完整性边界。\n- 将潜在攻击归约为单一、可记录和可审计的确认决策，便于人类与 LLM 验证器协同把关。\n- 支持多个智能体安全共享和复用知识，为测试时自我改进及社区级记忆治理提供架构参考。\n- 展示社区规模增长时降低毒化知识晋升和良性知识误淘汰风险、且不增加单用户验证负担的可能路径。"
notes = "标题：PerMIT: Securing Persistent Memory for LLM Agents via Machine-Checked Integrity Theorems and Scalable Community Trust\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28987\n摘要：Persistent memory is a largely undefended surface for injection and poisoning, and the same risk limits memory sharing among agents. We present PerMIT, a memory architecture with provable integrity guarantees under an explicit trust assumption: only user-typed inputs and values confirmed at a gate are trusted. PerMIT mediates the release and use of untrusted environment-derived values through a finite memory interface, while preserving agent-driven multi-turn planning and tool choice. Over this finite interface, we prove two integrity theorems that are also machine-checked in Lean 4, which together ensure that environment-derived memory reaches a sensitive action only if confirmed at the gate and influences the agent's behavior only through what the gate displays. Every attack in our threa"
